### [1] [Adversarial Style Optimization: Enhancing VLM Jailbreaks by GRPO-based Stylistic Triggers Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21619)
*Bingjun Luo,Jialin Guo,Yue Yao,Xinpeng Ding*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现多模态大语言模型存在理解能力与安全能力之间的风格不一致性，并提出对抗性风格优化方法(ASO)，通过微调图像编辑模型叠加优化后的风格修改，利用GRPO和分层奖励函数，显著提升现有视觉越狱攻击的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于内容的越狱攻击对快速进化的MLLMs效果不一致且表现不佳，未能利用非内容层面的漏洞。作者发现MLLMs存在风格不一致性，即模型能稳健理解各种视觉风格的内容，但防御机制容易被特定风格触发绕过。

Method: 提出ASO（对抗性风格优化），是一种即插即用的增强模块。通过GRPO智能体微调图像编辑模型，对给定的对抗图像叠加优化后的风格修改，并结合基于logit的明确拒绝检测信号和强大的裁判模型的高保真语义评估的结构化分层奖励函数。

Result: 大量实验表明，ASO显著提升了最先进攻击方法的攻击成功率(ASR)，证明了风格偏差是红队测试MLLMs的可扩展向量。

Conclusion: MLLMs的风格不一致性是一个重要的安全漏洞，ASO方法有效利用了这一漏洞，为多模态大语言模型的安全红队测试提供了新的可扩展途径。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved impressive performance, but their safety alignment remains vulnerable to jailbreak attacks. Existing content-based jailbreaks are often inconsistent and show unsatisfying performance against the rapidly evolving MLLMs, failing to exploit non-content-based vulnerabilities. Unlike previous research, we empirically find that MLLMs exhibit a Stylistic Inconsistency between their comprehension ability and safety ability: MLLMs can robustly understand content regardless of visual style, yet their defense mechanisms can be easily bypassed by specific stylistic triggers. Based on this finding, we propose Adversarial Style Optimization (ASO), a plug-and-play enhancement module to amplify existing visual jailbreaks. ASO fine-tunes an image-editing model to superimpose an optimized stylistic modification onto a given adversarial image, using a Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) agent guided by a Structurally-Tiered Reward Function that combines a logit-based signal for detecting explicit refusals with a high-fidelity semantic evaluation from a powerful judge model. Extensive experiments show that ASO significantly enhances the ASR of SOTA attacks, demonstrating that stylistic biases are a scalable vector for red-teaming MLLMs. Our code is available at https://github.com/bingjunluo/ASO.

</details>


### [2] [A Consensus-Based Framework for Relative Preference Evaluation of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21632)
*Mohtashim Khan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于共识的评估框架，通过多模型匿名投票和相对偏好排序（RII指标）来替代传统静态基准，用于衡量LLM在存在多个可接受答案时的响应质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM基准依赖静态数据集和客观评分，无法区分多个可接受答案之间的质量差异（如清晰度、完整性和有用性），需要一种能捕捉相对偏好的评估方法。

Method: 采用多模型共识评估框架：让五个不同LLM对同一提示词生成匿名响应，并通过结构化投票过程相互排序，聚合为相对智能指数（RII），以模型间的一致性作为响应质量的代理信号。

Result: 发现跨领域存在一致的偏好模式，某些模型的响应更频繁地被其他模型评为高质量，表明模型间存在偏好对齐现象。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种可扩展、模型驱动的相对评估方法，适用于存在多个有效答案的场景。虽不等同于人类评估，但聚合模型偏好可作为部分相关性的代理信号。

Abstract: Traditional benchmarks for LLMs primarily rely on static datasets and objective scoring metrics, which often fail to capture differences in response quality when multiple answers are acceptable. In such settings, correctness alone is insufficient to distinguish between responses that vary in clarity, completeness, and usefulness.
  This paper introduces a consensus-based evaluation framework that measures relative preference among model-generated responses rather than absolute correctness. Instead of evaluating outputs against a fixed ground truth, we assess how a panel of diverse LLMs ranks anonymized candidate responses to the same prompt. This approach treats aggregate inter-model agreement as a proxy for perceived response quality under blind conditions.
  We conduct a controlled study using five state-of-the-art LLMs across multiple domains, including programming, general knowledge, safety, logical reasoning, and mathematics. Each model generates responses and independently ranks peer outputs through a structured voting process. Scores are aggregated into a Relative Intelligence Index (RII), representing how frequently a model's responses are preferred by other models.
  Our findings reveal consistent preference patterns across domains, with certain models more frequently ranked highly by their peers. However, we emphasize that these results reflect inter-model preference alignment rather than objective correctness or human judgment. This framework provides a scalable, model-driven method for comparative evaluation, offering an alternative perspective on response quality in scenarios where multiple valid answers exist. While not directly aligned with human evaluation, prior work suggests that aggregated model preferences can partially correlate with human judgments, motivating this as a proxy signal.

</details>


### [3] [Evaluation design conditions the expert-vs-auto MeSH gap: a controlled comparison of bag-of-words and BiomedBERT on the Cohen benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21685)
*Samuel M. Okoe-Mensah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了专家标注与自动生成的MeSH词作为分类器特征的效果，发现评价设计（如交叉验证折数、语料规模）显著影响结论，在特定条件下两类特征的差距极小甚至消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统性综述中需要筛选大量摘要，MeSH词常被用作分类器特征，但专家标注与自动生成的MeSH词作为特征的优劣从未被直接比较，且评价设计的影响未被探究。

Method: 使用Cohen et al. (2006)药物类基准数据，在三个主题上运行词袋逻辑回归分类器（7次）和BiomedBERT（5个种子），通过改变交叉验证折数、语料规模等评价设计，分析专家与自动MeSH特征的性能差异。

Result: 在规范5折全语料设计下，词袋模型专家vs自动MeSH在Statins上差距为+0.096 WSS@95%；缩小语料至803篇时差距降至+0.033（CI含零）；10折全语料时降至+0.021（CI略不含零）。BiomedBERT在规范评价下差距为+0.020，与词袋10折结果相近。此外，15.1%的Statins输入在附加专家MeSH后超出BiomedBERT的512词限制。

Conclusion: 评价设计的变化会显著改变关于特征来源的基准测试结论；在变换器或10折词袋筛选流程中，测试主题上的差距约0.02 WSS@95%，CI至少一侧跨零；专家与自动MeSH的实际差异可能被高估。

Abstract: A systematic review begins with someone reading thousands of abstracts to identify the few that are relevant, and classifiers are used to prioritise that reading. Their inputs are often augmented with Medical Subject Headings (MeSH), assigned either by expert indexers weeks or months after publication or by automatic tools at once. To our knowledge the two have not been compared directly as classifier features, and no previous work has asked whether that comparison's outcome depends on how the classifier is evaluated. Using the Cohen et al. (2006) drug-class benchmark on three topics, we characterise a bag-of-words logistic regression classifier (seven reruns) and BiomedBERT (five seeds), then examine how the Statins result changes under alternative designs. Under the canonical 5-fold full-corpus design, the bag-of-words expert-vs-auto gap on Statins is +0.096 WSS@95%. Matching the corpus size to the smaller topics (n = 803) reduces it to +0.033 (95% bootstrap CI includes zero), and 10-fold cross-validation at full size to +0.021 (CI narrowly excludes zero). Under canonical evaluation BiomedBERT gives +0.020, within sampling noise of the bag-of-words 10-fold result. A power analysis indicates a Statins-sized effect would not have been detectable at the Opioids or ADHD variance, so those nulls are design-limited rather than informative. A representation asymmetry remains: 15.1% of Statins inputs exceed BiomedBERT's 512-token limit when expert MeSH terms are appended, so truncation may contribute to the smaller transformer gap, although this cannot be separated from training volume here. In screening pipelines using transformers or 10-fold bag-of-words, the gap on the topics tested is about 0.02 WSS@95%, with CIs spanning zero on at least one bound. More broadly, benchmark conclusions about feature sources can change substantially under reasonable changes to the evaluation design.

</details>


### [4] [Humanly: A Configurable and Traceable Environment for Human-AI Collaborative Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21758)
*Shenzhe Zhu,Haoqian Zhang,Xu Yang,Jingyu Tang,Yi Nian,Xiaoxue Du,Shu Yang,Alex Pentland,Joachim Baumann,Jiaxin Pei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Humanly是一个写作平台，通过记录完整的写作过程（包括人类打字、AI辅助等行为）生成可验证的写作证书，用于区分人类与AI的写作贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统写作评估只能看到最终文档，无法判断文档是由人类、AI还是人机协作完成的，现有的过程追踪工具存在局限。

Method: Humanly平台允许用户配置写作环境，在记录写作活动和AI辅助行为的工作空间中起草文档，并将完成的会话打包成包含配置感知异常行为审查的密封写作证书。

Result: 用户研究表明Humanly在不同角色中均有帮助；红队测试表明Humanly的打字检测器能够区分人类手动打字与自动打字。

Conclusion: Humanly通过记录写作过程而非仅依赖最终文本来解决写作来源的溯源问题，在课程作业、同行评审和个人认证等场景中有应用价值。

Abstract: Teachers, conference chairs, and public readers all judge writing from limited evidence, seeing only a finished document and not the process that produced it. Final text alone cannot reveal whether a document was produced through human typing, AI generation, or mixed human-AI collaboration. Existing process-tracking tools help, but many are tied to host-document histories, provide coarse activity records, and offer limited control over the writing environment. Humanly is a writing platform that makes the writing process itself the evidence. Users configure writing environments for personal documents or assigned tasks and draft in a workspace that records writing activity and in-platform AI assistance. Humanly can package a completed session into a sealed writing certificate with configuration-aware anomaly behavior review. It can support writing scenarios such as course assignments, peer review, and personal certification. Our user study shows that Humanly is helpful across roles, and a red-teaming study shows that the Humanly Typing Detector distinguishes human hand typing from automated typing.

</details>


### [5] [Probing Latent Colombian Identity Inferences in Qwen2.5-7B with Natural Language Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21774)
*Pablo Santiago Potes Velasco,María del Mar García Matabanchoy,Óscar Julián Pérez Ladino,Jhoan Stevan Mosquera Ortiz,Nicolás Lozano Mazuera,Gilber Alexis Corrales Gallego*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究使用自然语言自编码器（NLA）探索Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct模型在处理哥伦比亚-西班牙语和英语提示时，是否在激活层中内在地表征了哥伦比亚身份、社会经济地位或刻板印象信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型可能从微妙语言线索中推断人口统计属性，但针对西班牙语变体（尤其是哥伦比亚语境）的激活层可解释性研究尚属空白。

Method: 使用NLA对第20层残差流激活进行verbalize，覆盖每个提示的四个位置四分位数；数据集包含30个提示（15对西班牙语-英语配对），涵盖显性哥伦比亚线索、隐性哥伦比亚线索和中性控制。

Result: 报告了描述性比率和定性证据，而非统计显著性效应；重点考察潜在国籍或刻板印象表征是否在模型输出被verbalize之前就已出现。

Conclusion: 这项工作将激活层可解释性与对代表性不足的西班牙语变体的偏见评估联系起来。

Abstract: Large language models may infer demographic attributes from subtle linguistic cues even when those attributes are not explicitly stated. This pilot study examines whether Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct internally represents Colombian identity, socioeconomic status, or stereotype-related information when processing Colombian-Spanish and English prompts. We use Natural Language Autoencoders (NLA) to verbalize residual-stream activations from layer 20 across four positional quartiles per prompt. Our dataset contains 30 prompts arranged as 15 matched Spanish-English pairs, spanning explicit Colombian cues, implicit Colombian cues, and neutral controls. We report descriptive rates and qualitative evidence rather than statistically powered effects, focusing on whether latent nationality or stereotype representations appear before they are verbalized in the model output. This work connects activation-level interpretability with bias evaluation for underrepresented Spanish varieties.

</details>


### [6] [Khondo: A Multimodal Benchmark for Document Packet Splitting of Bangla Forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21780)
*Abu Tyeb Azad,Fahim Ahmed,Ishita Sur Apan,Ezharuddin Jubaer,Sumaiya Karim Katha,Armun Alam,Amin Ahsan Ali,Aman Chadha,Md Mofijul Islam,AKM Mahbubur Rahman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了Khondo，首个针对孟加拉国政府表格的文档包分割基准数据集，包含双语（孟加拉语-英语）和视觉原生特点。实验表明，多模态大语言模型在文档聚类方面表现尚可，但在恢复原始页面顺序方面存在困难，页面排序是主要挑战，语言是次要但一致的影响因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 政府和管理工作流中常见的文档包（多个文档拼接成单个文件）拆分为原始文档的任务非常困难，尤其是在低资源语言场景下。现有数据集多基于英文和OCR文本，缺乏针对低资源语言的双语视觉原生基准。

Method: 构建了Khondo数据集，包含5种拼接方案（从顺序到完全打乱）、14个行政领域，并提供边界标签、领域类型和页面顺序信息。对多模态大语言模型进行零样本评估，并通过两组受控分析（改变提示指令和文档语言）探究性能瓶颈。

Result: MLLMs在页面聚类方面表现尚可，但在恢复原始页面顺序方面存在显著困难。显式的页面顺序指令是必要但不充分的；英文文档包的排序比孟加拉语更可靠。页面排序是主要挑战，语言是次要但一致的影响因素。

Conclusion: Khondo确立了页面顺序重建作为基于视觉的低资源文档理解中的一个关键开放问题，并提供了一个可控基准来衡量该问题的解决进展。

Abstract: Document packets, multiple documents concatenated into a single file, are common in government and administrative workflows, yet splitting them into their constituent documents is difficult, especially for low-resource languages. We introduce Khondo (Bangla for split/segment), the first benchmark for document packet splitting on Bangladeshi government forms. Unlike prior English and OCR-text-based datasets, Khondo is bilingual (Bangla--English) and vision-native; where models operate directly on page images. It spans five concatenation schemes, from sequential to fully shuffled, across 14 administrative domains, with ground-truth boundaries, domain types, and page order. Zero-shot evaluation of MLLMs shows they cluster pages into their source documents fairly well but struggle in restoring the original page order once shuffled. To isolate what drives this difficulty, we run two controlled analyses, varying the prompt instruction and then the packet language. Both primarily affect ordering rather than clustering: (a) explicit page-order instructions are necessary but insufficient, and (b) English packets are ordered more reliably than Bangla, making page arrangement the dominant challenge and language a secondary but consistent factor. Khondo establishes page-order reconstruction as a key open problem in vision-based, low-resource document understanding, and provides a controlled benchmark for measuring progress toward solving it. Our dataset and code is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/Mausul/khondo

</details>


### [7] [Agentic Evaluation of Copyright Law Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21799)
*Zheng Hui,Doni Bloomfield,Noam Kolt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Copyright-Bench基准，用于评估LLM代理在商业任务中的版权法合规性。实验发现，即使存在公有领域替代品，LLM代理仍会选择侵权内容，且开放权重模型在用户偏好和时间压力下违规率上升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏评估LLM代理在实际商业任务中是否遵守版权法的框架，需要建立系统的评估方法。

Method: 构建Copyright-Bench基准，包含网站开发、商品设计和演示文稿制作三类商业任务，代理需在公有领域内容（合法）和受版权保护内容（侵权）之间选择；引入不同用户偏好提示和时间压力两种变量。

Result: (1) LLM代理在存在公有领域替代品的情况下仍选择受版权保护作品；(2) 开放权重模型在特定用户偏好和模拟时间压力下违规率增加。

Conclusion: LLM代理在商业任务中存在版权侵权风险，且开放权重模型对用户偏好和时间压力更为敏感，需要加强合规性设计。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly perform commercial tasks that involve retrieving external content such as images and, where appropriate, reproducing that content. LLM agents should comply with the law, including copyright law. Presently, however, we lack adequate frameworks to assess whether they do so in practice. To that end, we introduce \textbf{Copyright-Bench}, a benchmark designed to evaluate \textit{LLM agents' compliance with} \emph{copyright law}. Copyright-Bench is comprised of realistic commercial tasks---website development, merchandise design, and pitch deck production---that involve agents selecting between public-domain content (the use of which is \textit{legal}) and copyrighted content (the use of which is \textit{infringing} in this setting).The evaluation introduces prompt variations that simulate different user preferences, as well as time pressure.Comparing state-of-the-art LLM agents against a human baseline, we find that: (1) agents select copyrighted works despite the availability of public-domain alternatives; and (2) for open-weights models, violation rates increase in response to certain user preferences and simulated time pressure.

</details>


### [8] [Data Quality over Capacity: Internalizing Documents into LoRA Adapters for Closed-Book QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21861)
*Joan Figuerola Hurtado*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究将文档直接烘焙到4-bit Gemma-4-e4b模型的LoRA权重中，实现封闭式问答。核心发现：数据质量远超架构调整，一次数据整理将准确率从57.7%提升至85.7%；适配器容量是硬门槛；内部化方法在15文档测试中超越BM25-RAG基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索将文档知识直接编码到模型权重中，实现无需检索和上下文窗口的封闭式问答系统，降低推理延迟和计算成本。

Method: 使用LoRA对4-bit Gemma-4-e4b模型进行微调，将文档直接烘焙到权重中。通过约100次训练实验，系统研究适配器容量、数据质量、LoRA秩、学习率等变量的影响。

Result: 数据质量是封闭式准确率的主导因素；一次数据整理（缩短答案、删除琐碎内容）将15文档准确率从57.7%提升至85.7%；内部化方法在15文档测试中达84.2%召回率，优于BM25-RAG的58.9%和gold-chunk oracle的65.6%。

Conclusion: 数据质量比架构调整更重要；适配器容量是硬性门槛；内部化方法在中小规模语料上可替代检索增强生成；研究过程揭示了三种初期误诊，作为LLM训练调试的案例研究。

Abstract: We study baking documents directly into the weights of a 4-bit Gemma-4-e4b model via LoRA, so a system can answer questions about a corpus closed-book: no retrieval and no context-window budget. Across roughly 100 training runs from single documents to a 99-document corpus, we find that once adapter capacity is adequate, training-data quality is the dominant lever on closed-book accuracy, outweighing LoRA rank, learning rate, and two alternative architectures combined; capacity itself is a hard gate below which no data intervention helps. A single curation pass (shortening gold answers to canonical 1-6 word spans and dropping trivia) moved closed-book accuracy from 57.7% to 85.7% on a 15-document corpus, a larger jump than any architectural change. We confirm a capacity trend (rank must grow with corpus size) entangled with a coupling between rank and learning rate that we initially misdiagnosed. On a 15-document slice we add a real retrieval baseline: the internalized adapter (84.2% recall) beats a BM25-RAG pipeline with a base reader (58.9%) and even a realistic gold-chunk oracle (65.6%) at lower latency. We report the full arc, including three misdiagnoses, as a case study in debugging LLM training empirically.

</details>


### [9] [Leveraging External Knowledge for Historical Document Restoration via Retrieval-Augmented Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21936)
*Gabeen Kim,Kyeongpil Kang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出基于检索增强生成（RAG）的框架ARI，利用大语言模型结合外部知识检索，有效恢复历史文档中受损的命名实体，在韩语历史文档上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于掩码语言建模的修复方法虽能利用局部上下文，但无法恢复需要外部历史知识的命名实体，限制了历史文档修复的准确性。

Method: 提出ARI框架，结合预训练大语言模型的隐式知识与检索增强生成（RAG）的显式外部上下文，实现对上下文依赖专有名词的有效推理。

Result: 在韩语历史文档上，ARI在恢复一般字符和命名实体两方面均显著优于基线方法，专家评估也证实其作为实用工具的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法有望加速历史记录的分析工作，为领域专家提供实用的文档修复工具。

Abstract: Historical documents act as invaluable knowledge archives but often suffer from illegibility due to physical deterioration and damage. While existing restoration methods based on masked language modeling effectively utilize local context, they struggle to restore named entities that require external historical knowledge. To address this limitation, we introduce a novel framework for historical document restoration that leverages large language models with retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). By combining the implicit knowledge of pre-trained LLMs with explicitly retrieved external context, our model ARI effectively mitigates the challenge of inferring context-dependent proper nouns. Extensive experiments on Korean historical documents demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms baselines, achieving substantial gains in restoring both general characters and named entities. Furthermore, comprehensive evaluations including expert assessments confirm that ARI serves as a practical tool for domain experts, promising to accelerate the analysis of historical records.

</details>


### [10] [On Improving Faithfulness of Podcasts from Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21961)
*Soumya Dutta,Tejas Indulal Dhamecha,Pannaga Shivaswamy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了文档生成播客中的忠实性问题，提出了评估框架catch-n-repair方法，能有效检测并修正不忠实内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型生成播客时常引入无依据信息，但目前缺乏对长篇幅、多说话人、跨对话轮次的忠实性系统研究。

Method: 构建了涵盖5个领域、1500多份文档的数据集，采用多LLM生成播客文本，提出基于LLM-as-a-judge的逐轮评估框架，并设计了catch-n-repair检测与重写框架。

Result: 即使是GPT-4o等最先进模型也频繁产生无依据内容；catch-n-repair在域内和跨域场景中均一致提升了忠实性。

Conclusion: catch-n-repair是有效的模型无关方法，能在保持对话流畅性的同时提升文档生成播客的忠实性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate long-form conversational content such as podcasts from textual sources. While these systems produce fluent and engaging narratives, they often introduce ungrounded information. In this work, we present the first systematic study of faithfulness in document-grounded podcast generation, where grounding must be maintained across conversational turns in long-form, multi-speaker transcripts. We construct a dataset of over 1500 documents spanning five domains and generate podcast transcripts using multiple LLMs. We introduce a turn-level LLM-as-a-judge framework for evaluating whether conversational turns are supported by the source document, and validate its reliability through human studies. Our analysis shows that even state-of-the-art models, including GPT-4o, frequently generate ungrounded content. To mitigate this issue, we propose catch-n-repair, a model-agnostic framework that detects and rewrites unfaithful conversational turns while preserving conversational flow. Experiments demonstrate consistent improvements in faithfulness across both in-domain and out-of-domain settings.

</details>


### [11] [Ground Truth First: A Longitudinal Evaluation Instrument for Agent Memory, and the Tenure Crossover in Memory-Architecture Rankings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21962)
*Quentin Spencer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种逆向流水线方法，先设定事实脚本再生成文本，构建了包含有效期、信任区分等特征的综合基准，并评估了五种记忆架构，发现记忆系统排名随历史长度反转，最终发布了开源库Veracium。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体记忆基准存在标签错误、数据污染问题，且主要衡量短交互历史，缺乏对长周期、多维度记忆特征的系统评估。

Method: 采用逆向流水线：种子生命脚本采样器先发射事实（含有效期、波动类、来源通道），LLM渲染器生成聊天和邮件，保真验证器确认事实，机械式生成问题。构建包含15种类型、约380个问题的语料库，包含有效期、信任区分、注入探针和按日期查询集。

Result: 五种记忆架构排名随历史长度反转：预算化地图在3周领先但9周召回率从96%降至72%，而溯源类型图升至90%。全渲染历史基线在短周期持平或最优但9周无优势。写入质量与下游质量强相关，注入抵抗力取决于溯源边界是否保留。

Conclusion: 分层架构在两种机制下表现最佳（短周期96.8%），发布为开源库Veracium，包含语料生成器和测试框架，为LLM智能体记忆评估提供了更可靠、更全面的基准。

Abstract: Benchmarks for LLM-agent memory typically generate conversations first and extract answer keys afterwards -- with documented label-error and contamination problems -- and they overwhelmingly measure short interaction histories. We invert the pipeline: a seeded life-script sampler emits facts with validity intervals, volatility classes, and source channels before any text exists; an LLM renderer writes chat and email from per-event fact manifests; a fidelity verifier confirms every planted fact; and questions are instantiated mechanically from the script, so gold answers are script-valid by construction and separately validated for answerability. The synthetic, fictionalized corpus (~380 questions, 15 types) embeds features absent from the benchmarks we survey: per-fact validity intervals, sent/received trust distinctions, injection probes in a benign harness, and as-of-date question sets. Benchmarking five memory architectures against a no-memory control (fixed answerer, versioned LLM judge, three replicates, two horizons), we find backend rankings invert with history length: the budgeted curated-map memory that leads at three weeks loses recall of evicted content by nine weeks (96% to 72%) while a provenance-typed graph rises to 90%; the inversion is positive for all six users under complete cross-family re-judging (exact p=0.031). A full-rendered-history baseline ties or exceeds the best memory system at the short horizon but shows no judge-independent advantage at nine weeks, at about twice the read cost. Write-stage quality strongly correlates with downstream quality (weakly-written facts fail 24% vs 2%), and injection resistance tracked whether provenance boundaries survive representation. A layered architecture performs best among the memory systems in both regimes (96.8% short-horizon) and is released as Veracium, an open-source library, with the corpus generator and harness.

</details>


### [12] [MoE$^2$-LoRA: When MoE Models Meet MoE-style Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21978)
*Qingyu Yang,Haonan He,Minglei Li,Jingqi Ye,Tao Chen,Lei Bai,Peng Ye*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MoE²-LoRA方法，通过双通道路由条件投影模块和全局LoRA专家池，实现MoE模型的高效参数微调，在保持通用能力的同时取得最优下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE模型的参数高效微调方法存在两个问题：要么使用统一适配器忽略路由先验，降低效率并存在遗忘风险；要么依赖静态专家选择，限制了每token容量和跨专家特征学习。本文旨在解决这些问题。

Method: 提出MoE²-LoRA方法，包含两个核心设计：(1) 双通道路由条件投影(RCP)模块，复用基座路由器激活信息指导LoRA路由；(2) 全局共享的单一LoRA专家池，实现全模型范围的适配，并涌现出层次亲和性和均衡的专家利用。

Result: 在多种规模和专家粒度的MoE骨干模型上评估，MoE²-LoRA一致达到最优的下游任务准确率，同时保持了更强的通用能力。

Conclusion: MoE²-LoRA同时具备了先验复用、动态适配器路由和模型范围知识共享的优势，为MoE模型的高效微调提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures have been widely adopted in large language models, yet parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) for MoE models remains underexplored. Existing PEFT methods for MoE either ignore router priors with uniform adapters, reducing efficiency and risking forgetting, or rely on static expert selection, limiting per-token capacity and cross-expert feature learning. In this paper, we make the first attempt to fine-tune MoE models with MoE-style low-rank adaptation: our method, entitled MoE$^2$-LoRA, deeply couples the pretrained expert specialization with task-specific adaptivity via a dual-channel Routing-Conditioned Projection (RCP) module, which reuses base router activations to inform LoRA routing. We further introduce a single global LoRA expert pool shared across all layers, enabling model-wide adaptation with emergent layer-wise affinities and balanced expert utilization. MoE$^2$-LoRA simultaneously benefits from the advantages of prior reuse, dynamic adapter routing, and model-wide knowledge sharing. Evaluated on multiple MoE backbones with varying scales and expert granularities, MoE$^2$-LoRA consistently achieves state-of-the-art downstream accuracy while retaining stronger general capabilities.

</details>


### [13] [Analyzing Toxic Behavior and Its Impact on the Mastodon Community](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21980)
*Pasan Kamburugamuwa,Scrivner,Olga B*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了Mastodon去中心化社交平台中毒性内容的检测与缓解挑战，利用机器学习方法分析用户帖子，揭示了毒性趋势及其对社区健康和去中心化治理的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mastodon作为去中心化、独立审核的社交服务器联盟，缺乏统一审核标准，生态系统多样且不平衡，这为毒性内容的检测与缓解带来了独特挑战。

Method: 采用机器学习方法对Mastodon用户的帖子进行毒性内容的检测与分析。

Result: 研究揭示了Mastodon平台上毒性内容的发源与传播趋势，明确了毒性对社区健康的影响。

Conclusion: 该研究为去中心化社交平台中毒性内容治理提供了数据支撑，指出了当前治理模式的局限性与改进方向。

Abstract: Mastodon as a decentralized federation of independently moderated social servers poses unique challenges for the detection and mitigation of toxic content. There are no unified moderation standards. The ecosystem is very diverse and uneven. This paper explores the development and spread of toxicity in Mastodon, utilizing machine learning methods to examine user posts. The results offer clarity on toxicity trends and its implications for community health and decentralized governance.

</details>


### [14] [J-CoT: Chain-of-Thought in J-Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21981)
*Junde Wu,Jiayuan Zhu,Fengling Liu,Minhao Hu,Jiazhen Pan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出J-CoT，一种基于词汇索引坐标系统（J-space）的循环推理框架，在计算边界处传递词汇索引系数（J-thought）而非完整隐藏状态或自然语言，兼顾了语言的语义基础与隐式表示的灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言作为唯一循环接口过度受限，许多暂态计算无需完全用语言表达；现有的隐式推理方法传递完整稠密向量，缺乏选择和组织下一步所需信息的显式机制。

Method: 在模型隐藏空间中构建词汇索引坐标系（J-space），每个推理循环内在完整隐藏空间计算，在循环边界处将中间状态表达为词汇索引系数（J-thought），携带这些系数进入下一循环并映射回隐藏表示。

Result: 在匹配骨干网络和推理设置下，J-CoT-Zero在所有基准上达到或超过最强隐式推理基线，J-CoT-Train在数学、科学、编码和结构化路径推理任务上取得最高分。

Conclusion: J-CoT既不需要流畅的中间推理文本，也不需要循环传递完整隐藏状态，在多种推理任务上展现出优越性能。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought prompting improves language-model reasoning by carrying intermediate states across successive computation steps. However, relying on natural language as the only recurrent interface is overly restrictive, since many transient computations do not need to be fully verbalized. Existing latent-reasoning methods remove this constraint by recurrently propagating continuous hidden states. However, these methods pass a dense hidden vector as a whole, without an explicit mechanism for selecting and organizing the information needed by the next reasoning step. This motivates an intermediate interface that remains linguistically grounded without requiring a decoded sentence. We introduce \textbf{J-CoT}, a recurrent reasoning framework built on \emph{J-space}, a vocabulary-indexed coordinate system within the model's hidden representations. Within each cycle, the model computes in its full hidden space. At the cycle boundary, J-CoT expresses the intermediate state as vocabulary-indexed coefficients, carries these coefficients forward as a \emph{J-thought}, and maps them back into the model's hidden representation for the next cycle. J-CoT therefore requires neither a fluent intermediate rationale nor recurrence over the complete hidden state. Under matched backbone and inference settings, J-CoT-Zero matches or exceeds the strongest evaluated latent-reasoning baseline on every benchmark, while J-CoT-Train obtains the highest score across the evaluated mathematical, scientific, coding, and structured path-reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [15] [Analysing Self-Harm Representations in Language Models: a Cross-Architecture Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21988)
*Luis Espinosa-Anke,Carla Perez-Almendros*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文分析了LLM如何表征自伤内容，发现自伤信息在模型最后3-7%的层中结晶，并揭示了最准确的探针不一定具有最佳线性可分离性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自伤内容检测是NLP中的高难度、高风险任务，需要极高准确率以支持及时干预。理解LLM如何表征自伤内容，对于下游的自伤检测、LLM干预及内容治理具有重要意义。

Method: 使用两个自伤数据集（X-Sensitive和SH-Detection）和四个LLM，进行两项实验：(1) 在各模型所有层训练并评估线性探针；(2) 提取对比自伤方向并进行归一化分析。

Result: 自伤信息在模型最后3-7%的网络层（93-97%深度）中结晶；最准确的探针并不一定具有最佳的线性可分离性；Gemma-3-4B以更复杂的方式表征对比自伤方向。

Conclusion: LLM对自伤内容的表征集中在深层网络中，且不同模型的表征方式存在差异，这对自伤检测和LLM安全治理具有指导意义。

Abstract: Self-harm content is particularly challenging to detect using NLP techniques, and is also a high-stakes task which requires the highest accuracy to enable timely intervention or flagging at-risk users. We therefore present an analysis of how LLMs represent such self-harm content, which has downstream applications in self-harm detection, LLM intervention and governance and policing. In this paper, we focus on two datasets and four models, and perform two main experiments: (1) We train and evaluate linear probes across all layers of each model on two self-harm datasets: X-Sensitive and SH-Detection. Across both corpora, self-harm information crystallizes in the final 3 - 7% of network layers (93 to 97% depth). (2) We extract contrastive self-harm directions and, after performing a normaliation step, we find that the most accurate probes are not necessarily the most linearly separable. In particular, we find Gemma-3-4B to represent this \textit{contrastive self-harm direction} in a slightly different, more intricate way than the other LLMs.

</details>


### [16] [DWT-Fusion: A Signal-Based Framework for Training-Free LLM-Generated Text Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22026)
*Mehmet Batuhan Özdaş,Murat Osmanoğlu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DWT-Fusion框架，利用离散小波分析对代理模型的token级对数概率序列进行多分辨率分析，结合校准加权投票融合，实现了无需训练即可检测LLM生成文本，在HC3、M4和MAGE数据集上AUROC分别达到0.9919、0.8477和0.7471。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无训练检测方法依赖全局统计指标，忽略了token级预测概率中蕴含的局部和多尺度变化信息，导致检测性能受限。

Method: 对代理因果语言模型输出的token级对数概率序列进行离散小波变换（DWT）多分辨率分析，提取局部概率动态信号作为检测特征；进一步提出四种无训练投票变体（等权硬投票、等权软投票、校准加权硬投票、校准加权软投票）融合不同小波配置。

Result: 在HC3、M4和MAGE数据集上，最佳单小波配置AUROC分别为0.9872、0.8185、0.7138；采用校准加权投票融合后，AUROC分别提升至0.9919、0.8477、0.7471。

Conclusion: 基于DWT的多分辨率评分与校准引导投票融合方法，为无训练条件下LLM生成文本检测提供了有效且可解释的信号方案。

Abstract: Detecting LLM-generated text remains challenging under zero-shot and training-free conditions, especially when detectors must generalize across datasets, domains, and unseen generators. While existing training-free approaches exploit language-model statistics as detection signals, they typically characterize a text through global measures that summarize overall model behavior. Consequently, potentially informative local and multiscale variations in token-level predictability may remain underutilized. Motivated by this observation, we introduce DWT-Fusion, a training-free signal-based framework for detecting LLM-generated text using discrete wavelet analysis of token-level log-probability sequences produced by a proxy causal language model. The proposed framework analyzes these sequences through wavelet-based multiresolution signal representations and derives detection signals from localized probability dynamics. We further evaluate four training-free voting variants, including equal-weight hard voting, equal-weight soft voting, calibration-weighted hard voting, and calibration-weighted soft voting, to combine multiple wavelet configurations without training a supervised meta-classifier. We evaluate the framework on HC3, M4, and MAGE using GPT-Neo-2.7B, GPT-J-6B, Falcon-7B, and LLaMA-3-8B as proxy models. The best single wavelet configurations achieve AUROC values of 0.9872, 0.8185, and 0.7138 on HC3, M4, and MAGE, respectively. With calibration-weighted voting, the best ensemble variants further improve AUROC to 0.9919, 0.8477, and 0.7471. These findings show that DWT-based multiresolution scoring and calibration-guided voting fusion provide effective and interpretable signals for training-free LLM-generated text detection.

</details>


### [17] [Enough is as good as a feast: A Comprehensive Analysis of How Reinforcement Learning Mitigates Task Conflicts in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22039)
*Zixuan Ren,Jinliang Lu,Junhong Wu,Yang Zhao,Dai Dai,Hua Wu,Haifeng Wang,Chengqing Zong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究系统探索了强化学习（RL）训练的大语言模型在模型合并中的表现，发现相较于传统监督微调（SFT），RL显著减少了任务冲突和合并后的性能下降，使RL训练模型更适合合并。通过实验和理论分析，揭示了三个关键因素：on-policy训练数据控制梯度更新幅度、RL优化目标逐步减少冲突参数更新、正负例联合优化防止参数冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型合并研究主要关注合并策略本身，而不同训练范式（如SFT和RL）对合并效果的影响尚未被充分探索，因此本研究旨在系统比较RL训练与SFT训练的模型在合并中的行为差异及原因。

Method: 通过在五个代表性任务上进行全面评估，对比RL训练和SFT训练模型的合并表现；并辅以大量实证实验和理论分析，从三个维度（训练数据、优化目标、正负例优化）探究RL更适合合并的深层原因。

Result: RL训练模型在合并后任务冲突显著减少，性能下降更小；RL的on-policy训练数据使梯度更新幅度更小，避免了覆盖已有知识；RL的"够用即丰盛"优化目标随模型收敛逐步减少冲突参数更新；正负例联合优化将模型导向无偏的任务特定参数子空间，防止参数冲突。

Conclusion: RL训练的大语言模型因其独特的训练特性，在模型合并中具有天然优势，是比SFT更优的合并候选范式，为未来多模型合并提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Model merging plays a crucial role in consolidating multiple specialized models into a single, unified model, especially in the era of large language models (LLMs). Recent research has primarily focused on developing strategies to enhance merging performance with the trained models, while the impact of training paradigms, such as supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL), on the effectiveness of model merging remains underexplored. In this study, we systematically explore the merging behavior of RL-trained LLMs compared to those trained with traditional SFT. Through comprehensive evaluations across five representative tasks, we find that RL significantly reduces task conflicts and results in less performance degradation after merging, making RL-trained models particularly well-suited for this process. To unearth the reasons behind the superior suitability of RL for model merging, we conduct extensive empirical experiments and theoretical analyses. Our findings highlight three key factors: (1) On-policy training data in RL control the gradient updates in a smaller magnitude, reducing the risk of overwriting existing knowledge for other tasks in the model. (2) The RL optimization objective, which favors ``\textit{enough is as good as a feast}", progressively reduces the magnitude and the number of conflict parameter updates as the model converges. (3) Joint optimization of positive and negative examples in RL steers the model towards an unbiased task-specific parameter subspace, ensuring robust performance while further preventing parameter conflicts.

</details>


### [18] [Developing and Validating the Spanish Version of the Large Language Models Dependency Scale (LLM-D12-SP)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22041)
*Tran Gia Bao,Mo El-Haj,Sameha Al-Shakhsi,Antonio Garcia-Cabot,Raian Ali,Ala Yankouskaya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究首次验证了西班牙语版大语言模型依赖量表（LLM-D12-SP），通过对386名西班牙语参与者的分析，确认了量表的二维结构（工具性依赖和关系性依赖），显示出良好的内部一致性和区分效度，并与网络成瘾、信任度等外部变量相关，为跨语言研究LLM依赖提供了有效工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在组织和工作场景中的广泛应用，需要可靠且经过文化验证的工具来评估对LLM的心理依赖，尤其是在使用日益增长但心理测量工具匮乏的西班牙语人群中。

Method: 对386名西班牙语参与者（平均年龄28岁，55%男性）施测LLM-D12-SP，采用验证性因子分析检验双因子结构，通过Cronbach's alpha评估内部一致性，并分析区分效度和外部效度（与网络成瘾、LLM可信度、认知需求的关系）。

Result: 验证性因子分析支持原始的两因子结构；量表整体内部一致性良好（α=0.89），工具性依赖（α=0.86）和关系性依赖（α=0.85）分量表也表现良好；两个维度既相关又有区别；与网络成瘾和LLM可信度正相关，与认知需求弱相关或不相关。

Conclusion: 结合先前的英语和阿拉伯语验证，本研究建立了LLM-D12跨语言的结构支持，为西班牙语人群提供了一个心理测量学上可靠的LLM依赖评估工具，有助于在组织情境中研究LLM使用的心理方面。

Abstract: There is a growing need for reliable and culturally validated instruments to assess psychological dependency on large language models (LLMs), particularly as LLMs are increasingly used for task execution, decision-making, and communication in organizational and work-related settings. This need is especially relevant for Spanish-speaking populations, where LLM adoption is rapidly expanding, yet validated psychometric tools remain scarce. The present study reports the first validation of the Spanish version of the Large Language Model Dependency Scale (LLM-D12-SP), extending prior validations conducted in English- and Arabic-speaking samples. The LLM-D12 is a two-dimensional instrument assessing Instrumental Dependency (reliance on LLMs for performing tasks and supporting decisions) and Relationship Dependency (psychological reliance on LLMs for companionship and social interaction). A total of 386 Spanish-speaking participants (M = 28.0 years, SD = 6.1; 55% male) completed the LLM-D12-SP. Confirmatory factor analysis supported the original two-factor structure. The scale demonstrated good internal consistency (Cronbach's alpha = 0.89 total; 0.86 Instrumental; 0.85 Relationship). Discriminant validity analyses indicated that the two subscales represent related but distinct constructs. External validation showed that both dependency dimensions were positively associated with internet addiction and perceived trustworthiness of LLMs, while showing weak or no association with need for cognition. Together with prior English and Arabic validations, these findings establish cross-linguistic support for the scale's structure and provide a psychometrically sound tool for investigating psychological aspects of LLM use in organizational contexts.

</details>


### [19] [Scaling Native Multimodal Pre-Training From Scratch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22043)
*Haoyuan Wu,Aoqi Wu,Hai Wang,Jiajia Wu,Jinxiang Ou,Bei Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了原生多模态预训练（从零开始在多模态输入上训练模型）的缩放规律，发现存在可预测的计算最优模型大小和token数量，且语言与多模态目标表现出不同的缩放行为。语言分配律对数据组成不敏感，而多模态分配律高度敏感。研究还推导了效率边界，并证明原生多模态预训练能实现正向跨模态迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型语言模型虽然推理能力出色，但仅依赖文本预训练限制了其对多模态物理世界的感知。原生多模态预训练可避免此局限，但其缩放特性尚未被系统表征，因此需要填补这一空白。

Method: 在固定计算预算下，研究transformer视觉-语言模型的最优模型大小和token数量；通过计算最优模型大小和token数量的幂律缩放规律，分析语言与多模态目标的不同缩放行为，并建模数据组成对计算律和分配指数的影响。

Result: 最小目标损失遵循可预测的计算律；语言分配律对数据组成不敏感，多模态分配律高度敏感；文本密集型混合在更大模型尺度上才实现计算高效；推导出模型大小、token数量和数据混合的具体效率边界配置。

Conclusion: 该实证研究为可预测地缩放多模态基础模型奠定了关键基础，表明原生多模态预训练能增强纯文本空间推理并实现鲁棒的多模态上下文学习。

Abstract: Although large language models (LLMs) exhibit remarkable reasoning capabilities, their reliance on text-only pre-training restricts the perception of the multimodal physical world. Native multimodal pre-training avoids this limitation by training models from scratch on multimodal inputs, thereby achieving deep cross-modal integration and mitigating optimization asymmetries inherent to traditional late-fusion architectures. Despite these advantages, the scaling properties of this paradigm remain systematically uncharacterized. To address this gap, we investigate the optimal model size and token count for training a transformer-based vision-language model under a fixed computational budget. We demonstrate that minimal objective loss adheres to a predictable compute law, whereas compute-optimal model sizes and token counts scale as power laws. Notably, language and multimodal objectives manifest distinct scaling behaviors. The language allocation law is largely invariant to the composition of the data, indicating stable language learning regardless of the multimodal data ratio. Conversely, the multimodal allocation law is highly sensitive to this composition. Specifically, text-heavy mixtures become compute-efficient only at larger model scales, shifting the optimal resource allocation toward greater model capacity. Additionally, by modeling the influence of data composition on compute laws and allocation exponents, we derive an efficiency frontier specifying precise configurations of model size, token count, and data mixture. Downstream evaluations further reveal that native multimodal pre-training induces positive cross-modal transfer, thereby enhancing pure-text spatial reasoning and enabling robust multimodal in-context learning. In summary, this empirical research establishes the essential groundwork for predictably scaling multimodal foundation models.

</details>


### [20] [Benchmarking Fine-tuning and Retrieval Strategies for a Multimodal Language Model on the NRC Reactor Operator Licensing Examination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22067)
*Isak Hwang,Yoon Pyo Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估了310亿参数的多模态模型（Gemma 4 31B-IT）在美国核管理委员会反应堆操作员执照考试中的表现。采用监督微调（SFT）结合固定大小分块的检索增强生成（RAG）配置在14门考试中通过8门，聚合准确率达79.7%，而未微调的模型全部未通过。研究发现分块策略的选择取决于模型训练状态，且RAFT在匹配搜索环境方面不如标准SFT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在核电行业的应用需要基于领域特定知识的输出，但目前缺乏对LLMs能否达到核反应堆操作员级别知识水平的系统评估。

Method: 以2015-2021年3月期14份通用基础考试（7份压水堆、7份沸水堆）为基准，采用80%人类通过标准。比较基础模型与采用Gemini蒸馏思维链监督微调（SFT）、基于BM25稀疏检索的检索增强生成（RAG）以及检索增强微调（RAFT）的配置。在检索管道中比较固定大小滑动窗口分块与结构感知分块策略。

Result: SFT配置结合固定大小分块RAG在14门考试中通过8门，是所有配置中表现最佳的；无微调配置全部未通过考试。聚合准确率达79.7%，置信区间跨越阈值；压水堆项目准确率达80.2%。此外，分块策略的选择取决于模型训练状态，RAFT在匹配搜索环境方面不如标准SFT。

Conclusion: 研究证明了特定的微调与搜索方法组合能够达到操作员级别的能力水平，为LLMs在核电领域的应用提供了实践指导。

Abstract: The integration of large language models (LLMs) into the nuclear power industry requires outputs grounded in domain-specific knowledge. This study evaluates a 31-billion-parameter open-weight multimodal model (Gemma 4 31B-IT) on its capacity to apply nuclear knowledge by benchmarking eight model-retrieval configurations against the U.S. Nuclear Regulatory Commission (NRC) Reactor Operator licensing examination. We evaluate 14 Generic Fundamentals Examinations (GFE) from the 2015-2021 March sittings (seven pressurized and seven boiling water reactor exams) using the standard 80% human passing criterion. The base model is compared against configurations utilizing supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on Gemini-distilled chain-of-thought (CoT) rationales, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) with BM25 sparse retrieval over the U.S. Department of Energy Fundamentals Handbook, and retrieval-augmented fine-tuning (RAFT). Within the retrieval pipeline, we compare fixed-size sliding-window chunking against structure-aware chunking. The SFT configuration with fixed-size chunking RAG met the criterion on 8 of the 14 examinations, outperforming all alternatives, whereas no configuration without fine-tuning passed any. Aggregate accuracy reached 79.7%, with a confidence interval spanning the threshold, and 80.2% on PWR items specifically. Furthermore, two regularities emerged: the preferred chunking strategy reverses depending on the model's training state, and RAFT underperforms compared to standard SFT in matching search environments. These results demonstrate which combination of fine-tuning and search approaches achieves operator-level capabilities.

</details>


### [21] [FSE: Continual Learning for Named Entity Recognition by Fast-Slow Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22075)
*Yunan Zhang,Yang Fan,Heng Li,Xiangping Wu,Qingcai Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出FSE（Fast-Slow Experts）模型，通过共享快专家学习token级链接过滤无效跨度，任务特化慢专家对剩余候选进行跨度分类，并采用长度衰减负采样策略解决跨度不平衡问题，在CLNER任务上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习命名实体识别方法存在灾难性遗忘和任务间共享信息利用不足的问题。

Method: 提出FSE模型：1）共享快专家学习token级链接高效过滤非候选跨度；2）任务特化慢专家仅对剩余候选进行跨度分类；3）引入长度衰减负采样策略缓解跨度不平衡。

Result: 在OntoNotes和FewNERD合成数据集上达到SOTA性能，各组件有效，收敛更快，双专家功能符合预期。

Conclusion: FSE通过快慢专家分工机制有效平衡了知识共享（稳定性）与学习负担降低（可塑性），在持续学习NER场景中表现出色。

Abstract: Continual Learning for Named Entity Recognition (CLNER) enable models to incrementally learn new entity types without forgetting previously acquired ones. However, existing methods suffer from catastrophic forgetting and insufficient exploitation of shared information across tasks. This paper proposes FSE, a Fast-Slow Experts enhanced span-based NER model for CLNER. The shared fast expert learns token-level links to efficiently filter out unlikely spans, while the task-specific slow expert performs span classification only on the remaining candidates. It stabilizes learning by promoting knowledge sharing across tasks and maintains plasticity by reducing learning burden at each task. A length-decay negative sampling strategy to mitigate span imbalance is also introduced. Extensive experiments on OntoNotes and FewNERD synthestic datasets demonstrate that FSE achieves state-of-the-art performance in CLNER scenarios, with effectiveness of each component, empirical evidence of faster convergence and expected functionality of both experts.

</details>


### [22] [MEUSLI: a Multilingual Projector for LLM-based ASR and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22100)
*Lorenzo Concina,Seraphina Fong,Marco Matassoni,Alessio Brutti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MEUSLI是一个开源多语言投影器系列，将Whisper编码器与多语言大语言模型连接，支持28种欧洲语言的端到端ASR，并能扩展到其他任务如语音翻译和主题识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统仅支持少数语言且多限于英语，缺乏开源的多语言语音-语言模型连接方案。

Method: 将Whisper编码器与开源多语言LLM通过轻量级投影器连接，采用持续学习技术实现语言扩展。

Result: 在28种欧洲语言的ASR任务上表现强劲，支持高资源和低资源语言，并可扩展至语音翻译和主题识别任务。

Conclusion: MEUSLI为多语言语音理解任务提供了可扩展、包容性的开源SpeechLLM基础。

Abstract: Lightweight projectors are an established way to connect pre-trained speech encoders with large language models (LLMs), mapping acoustic features into token-level embeddings for tasks like ASR and spoken question answering. Existing systems, however, typically only support a few languages and are often limited to English. We introduce MEUSLI, the first open-science multilingual projector family that links a Whisper encoder with open-source multilingual LLMs, enabling fully open-source end-to-end ASR in 28 European languages. MEUSLI extends prior monolingual pipelines, delivering strong results across high- and low-resource languages. Using proper continual leaning techniques, MEUSLI can be easily extended to other languages not seen in training. We further demonstrate that the MEUSLI projector can be leveraged beyond ASR, enabling multilingual speech translation and topic identification with only a few hours of task specific supervision per language. Overall, MEUSLI provides a solid foundation for multilingual speech understanding tasks, supporting scalable and inclu- sive open-source SpeechLLM

</details>


### [23] [Dynamic Commonsense Coordination for Empathetic Response Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22136)
*Zhengyu Qi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出动态常识协调框架DCC，通过残差交互、关联过滤和迭代解码三个模块提升共情响应生成的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在理解和生成阶段使用固定常识表示，无法有效协调不同阶段的常识知识，限制了共情响应生成的效果。

Method: 提出DCC框架，包含三个模块：SCE-AttnRes（残差式常识交互）、AGCF（关联引导的常识过滤）、ICAD（迭代常识感知解码）。

Result: 在Empathetic-Dialogues基准上，相比CEM基线，DCC提升了情感分类准确率和响应多样性，困惑度相当；LLM盲评显示响应在相关性、连贯性和信息量上更优。

Conclusion: DCC通过动态协调常识知识，有效改善了共情响应生成的质量，验证了三模块联合设计的有效性。

Abstract: Empathetic Response Generation (ERG) requires models to recognize users' emotions and generate empathetic responses. Commonsense knowledge has been shown to support such reasoning, yet existing approaches typically reuse fixed commonsense representations across understanding and generation, limiting their ability to coordinate such knowledge across different stages. We propose DCC, a Dynamic Commonsense Coordination Framework with three complementary modules: residual-based commonsense interaction (SCE-AttnRes) to integrate contextual and situational commonsense representations, Association-Guided Commonsense Filtering (AGCF) to down-weight low-relevance commonsense relations, and Iterative Commonsense-Aware Decoding (ICAD) to dynamically retrieve commonsense memories during generation. Experiments on the Empathetic-Dialogues benchmark show that DCC improves emotion classification accuracy and response diversity over the CEM baseline while maintaining comparable perplexity. An LLM-based blind evaluation further demonstrates that DCC generates responses with better relevance, coherence, and informativeness. The code and implementation details will be publicly available at https://github.com/Hanabi-Q/DCC-ERG.

</details>


### [24] [From Isolated Tasks to Structured Capabilities: A Multilayer Taxonomy for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22182)
*Shixin Fang,Jiachen Wo,Wenjuan Qin,Sihang Jiang,Yanghua Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种14个能力域、91个子技能的多层分类法，将LLM评估从基于任务转向基于能力，并通过对15,934篇论文的映射分析揭示了研究注意力分布严重不均的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评估以任务为中心，缺乏基于能力维度的统一框架，导致跨研究难以比较、能力覆盖不明确、研究空白难以识别。

Method: 构建了涵盖原始层、建构层和整合层共14个能力域和91个子技能的多层分类法，以人类认知科学为指导定义能力；对2023-2025年间31,505篇论文进行筛选，最终对15,934篇LLM相关论文进行多模型标注、共识和仲裁分析。

Result: 语言语义能力（22.3%）和推理能力（21.3%）占据研究主导；6个能力域出现在不到2%的论文中；心理理论与社会推理交互虽然仅有62次共现，但提升度高达30.84，表明其高度特异性关联。

Conclusion: 该分类法将分析单元从孤立任务转向结构化能力，有助于研究组织、覆盖审计、评估解释以及为诊断、训练和迁移提供可检验假设。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) evaluation spans diverse tasks and benchmarks, yet evidence remains organized around tasks rather than the capabilities they probe. This fragmentation limits cross-study comparison, obscures capabilities tasks recruit, and makes coverage gaps difficult to identify.
  We introduce a multi-layer taxonomy of 14 capability domains and 91 subskills across Primitive, Constructed, and Integrative layers. Human cognitive science guides capability definition and organization, not LLM architecture. Layer assignments draw on developmental precedence and hypothesized functional support, while human-origin constructs are adapted to observable model behavior.
  To demonstrate operational utility, we screened 31,505 papers from ACL, AAAI, ICML, and NeurIPS between 2023 and 2025 and mapped 15,934 LLM-focused papers through multi-model annotation, consensus, and arbitration. Direct research attention concentrated on Language-Semantic Competence (3,551; 22.3%), Reasoning (3,388; 21.3%), Planning and Decision-Making (2,149; 13.5%), and Perception (1,954; 12.3%), whereas six domains appeared in fewer than 2% of papers. Within domains, the most frequent subskill had a median prevalence of 97.9% and appeared in at least 90% of papers in 10 of 14 domains. Language-Semantic Competence and Reasoning formed the highest-volume pair (n = 1,864; 11.7%; lift = 2.47), whereas Theory of Mind and Social Reasoning and Interaction showed the highest lift among pairs with at least 20 co-occurrences (n = 62; lift = 30.84).
  By shifting the unit of analysis from isolated tasks to structured capabilities, the taxonomy supports research organization, coverage audits, evaluation interpretation, and testable hypotheses for diagnosis, training, and transfer.

</details>


### [25] [Why Large Language Models and Humans Converge and Diverge in Evaluating Creativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22218)
*Pengzhao Lyu,Yeun Joon Kim,Hanlin Xiao,Yingyue Luna Luan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过三项研究发现，LLM在评估创造力时，与人类标准的一致性在新颖性维度最强，在需要社会、市场等背景信息的语境维度最弱；不同LLM依赖的标准差异很大，且评估结果与人类的实际一致性仅为中等水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM越来越多地被用作创造力评估工具，但其评估结果与人类评价的一致性证据不一，需要系统探究LLM何时及为何与人类判断趋同或分歧。

Method: 开展三项研究，使用六个广泛应用的LLM，首先识别LLM创造力评估所用的标准，然后分别用1103个和1195个创意评估其与人类评价的一致性和对上下文信息的敏感性。

Result: LLM依赖的标准范围比人类更窄；新颖性维度一致性最强，语境维度分歧最大；不同LLM有各自特定的评估标准；LLM评估与人类评价呈中等相关；标准更宽的LLM更好地区分创意高低；LLM对上下文信息不敏感。

Conclusion: LLM与人类评价的一致性取决于判断所需的证据类型和模型所用的标准；选择不同的LLM作为评估者会影响哪些创意被认为更有创造性。

Abstract: Despite the growing use of large language models (LLMs) as creativity evaluators, evidence of their alignment with human evaluations remains mixed, raising the question of when and why their judgments converge with or diverge from human judgments. Across three studies and six widely used LLMs, we addressed this gap by identifying the standards underlying LLM creativity evaluation and examining their downstream implications. Study 1 showed that LLMs generally relied on a narrower subset of human creativity evaluation standards. Convergence with human standards was strongest in the novelty dimension, whereas divergence was clearest in the contextual dimension, which captures social, market, and reputational information. Moreover, each LLM exhibited distinct, model-specific standards that varied substantially in breadth. These differences in evaluation standards were reflected in actual creativity judgments. Study 2 (N = 1,103 ideas) showed that LLM evaluations were moderately correlated with human evaluations, and individual LLMs with broader standards better distinguished ideas humans judged as more versus less creative. Study 3 (N = 1,195) showed that LLMs were less sensitive to contextual information: such information significantly altered human creativity ratings but left LLM ratings largely unchanged. Together, our findings help explain the mixed evidence on LLM-human alignment, showing that alignment depends on the evidence a judgment demands and the standards each model applies. LLMs may resemble humans when evaluations emphasize intrinsic qualities such as novelty, yet diverge when judgments require contextual information. Selecting an LLM evaluator is therefore a consequential decision: different models, applying different standards, recognize different ideas as creative.

</details>


### [26] [Biomedical Machine Translation for Low-Resource Arabic-Script Languages via Cross-Lingual Transfer and LoRA Adapter Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22300)
*Abdullah Alabdullah,Arash Eslamighayour,Sarp Harbalioglu,Lifeng Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文利用阿拉伯语和波斯语作为高资源枢轴语言，通过LoRA微调小规模解码器专用LLM，对四种低资源阿拉伯语系语言（达里语、普什图语、索拉尼库尔德语、乌尔都语）进行生物医学领域的跨语言迁移。研究发现，仅用500句监督适配即可使达里语达到接近枢轴语言的质量，而LoRA适配器合并在零数据成本下也能取得不错效果，但普什图语和索拉尼库尔德语仍不足以用于临床部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决阿拉伯语系语言在生物医学NMT资源极度匮乏的问题，探索利用高资源枢轴语言（阿拉伯语、波斯语）向四种低资源目标语言进行跨语言迁移的有效策略。

Method: 采用LoRA微调小规模解码器专用LLM，训练领域特定的枢轴适配器，评估三种迁移策略：少样本上下文学习、最小监督适配（500句）以及零数据LoRA适配器合并。

Result: 监督适配使达里语CHrF++达到41.01（接近枢轴质量），乌尔都语达到28.88；适配器合并对达里语达到监督适配的98.5%性能（差距3.5 CHrF++以内）；普什图语和索拉尼库尔德语效果不足。

Conclusion: LoRA适配器合并在语言结构相近时效果显著，即使没有目标语言生物医学数据也能工作；但跨语言迁移在结构差异过大时存在明显局限，难以满足高风险临床部署需求。

Abstract: We present a systematic study of healthcare-domain cross-lingual transfer to address the scarcity of biomedical NMT resources for Arabic-script languages. We use Arabic and Persian as higher-resource pivots to improve translation for \textbf{four severely low-resource} targets: Dari (Afghan Persian, a standardised variety of Persian), Pashto, Sorani Kurdish (Central Kurdish, a major standardized variety of Kurdish), and Urdu (closely related to Hindi). Using LoRA fine-tuning on small decoder-only LLMs, we train \textit{domain-specific pivot adapters} and evaluate \textbf{three transfer strategies}: few-shot in-context learning, minimal supervised adaptation, and, to the best of our knowledge, for the first time in this setting, zero-data LoRA adapter merging. Supervised adaptation with just 500 sentences achieves near pivot-language quality for Dari (CHrF++ 41.01) and meaningful gains for Urdu (28.88), while adapter merging reaches within 3.5 CHrF++ of supervised adaptation for Dari at zero additional cost. Pashto and Sorani Kurdish remain insufficient for high-stakes clinical deployment exposing the limits of cross-lingual transfer when structural distance from the pivots is too great. LoRA adapter merging works surprisingly well for closely related languages, even without target-language biomedical data.

</details>


### [27] [A Factorial Study of Synthetic Data Generation for Low-Resource Machine Translation using Grammar Books](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22376)
*Varun Ghat Ravikumar,Sina Ahmadi,Lena Jäger,Rico Sennrich*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出利用大语言模型从语法书籍中提取规则、例句和词典，生成合成平行语料来微调机器翻译模型，在三种低资源语言上取得了显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数濒危语言缺乏机器翻译所需的平行语料，但存在描述性语法书籍，需要一种方法来利用这些静态语言文档生成可用数据。

Method: 使用大语言模型从语法书中提取语法规则、例句和词典，生成合成平行语料用于微调翻译模型，代替推理时注入语法信息的方法。

Result: 在Kalamang、Tuatschin和Mandan三种语言上，微调合成数据在75%和59%的配置中优于基线，最佳ChrF++分别提升+8.8、+5.3和+3.3。

Conclusion: 静态语言文档可以转化为机器翻译微调数据，为严重低资源语言提供实用的翻译工具开发路径。

Abstract: Most endangered languages lack the parallel data required for machine translation, despite the existence of descriptive grammar books. We introduce a pipeline that uses large language models to extract grammatical rules, example sentences, and lexicons from grammar books and generate synthetic parallel corpora for fine-tuning-rather than feeding grammar content into prompts at inference time, as in prior work. Validated on three typologically diverse low-resource languages-Kalamang (Papuan), Tuatschin (Romance), and Mandan (Siouan)-we show that fine-tuning on synthetic data improves over seed-data baselines in 75% of configurations for Kalamang and 59% for Tuatschin, with best-case ChrF++ gains of +8.8, +5.3, and +3.3 respectively. Through a systematic factorial study across 96 configurations varying target part-of-speech, retrieval granularity, and sample volume, we identify which factor combinations drive gains and where they break down. Our results demonstrate that static linguistic documentation can be repurposed for machine translation fine-tuning, offering a practical path towards translation tools for severely under-resourced languages.

</details>


### [28] [grapheme-kit: Grapheme-Level Metrics and Text Processing for Multilingual NLP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22456)
*Izzath Nisfer,Ashini Kavindya,Ovindu Atukorala,Purushoth Velayuthan,Menan Velayuthan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 介绍grapheme-kit库，通过将文本距离/相似度/评估指标从Unicode码点扩展至grapheme clusters（特别是针对泰米尔语和僧伽罗语），实现对复杂文字系统更准确的OCR评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Unicode码点的词汇距离/相似度/评估指标在多种文字系统中，一个grapheme由多个码点表示时会产生错误的表征，亟需更精准的grapheme级度量。

Method: 开发开源Python库grapheme-kit，将传统指标扩展至grapheme clusters；为泰米尔语和僧伽罗语提供准确的grapheme聚类识别与组/分解工具。

Result: OCR案例研究表明，grapheme级指标对复杂文字提供了更可靠的评估结果。

Conclusion: grapheme-kit可有效弥补现有Unicode码点级指标在复杂文字系统中的不足，提升OCR等下游任务的评估准确性。

Abstract: Existing lexical distance, similarity, and evaluation metrics operate on Unicode code points, which can misrepresent errors in writing systems where a single grapheme is represented by multiple Unicode code points. We introduce grapheme-kit, an open-source Python library that extends these metrics to operate on grapheme clusters instead. The library also provides improved grapheme processing for Tamil and Sinhala, including accurate grapheme cluster identification and grapheme composition/decomposition utilities. Through an OCR case study, we demonstrate that grapheme-level metrics provide a more faithful evaluation of complex scripts.

</details>


### [29] [Skill Self-Play: Pushing the Frontier of LLM Capability with Co-Evolving Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22529)
*Siyuan Huang,Pengyu Cheng,Haotian Liu,Tao Chen,Yihao Liu,Jingwei Ni,Shijie Zhou,Ziyi Yang,Gangwei Jiang,Mengyu Zhou,Yu Cheng,Xiaoxi Jiang,Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Skill Self-Play（Skill-SP）框架，通过技能作为中介，在结构化验证与开放探索之间取得平衡，实现LLM的自我进化训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化方法面临任务多样性与验证可靠性之间的根本矛盾：环境约束方法反馈精确但领域狭窄，开放式自生成方法任务空间广但缺乏可靠验证。

Method: 提出Skill-SP共进化框架，包含提议者、求解者和动态技能控制器三个组件，通过强化学习循环实现：提议者根据动态采样技能生成挑战性任务，求解者探索候选方案，技能控制器收集执行反馈以更新和扩展技能库。

Result: 在工具使用和推理基准上的实验表明，Skill-SP作为稳健的进化引擎，持续提升强基座模型的性能上限，同时显著改善初始对齐不佳的模型表现。

Conclusion: 技能作为中介有效弥合了结构化验证与开放探索之间的鸿沟，Skill-SP框架通过技能的动态路由与共进化机制，为LLM自我进化训练提供了新的可行范式。

Abstract: LLM training is shifting from manual design and annotation to interaction-driven self-evolution. However, existing self-evolutionary methods face a fundamental dilemma between task diversity and verification reliability: environment-bound methods obtain precise feedback but confine learning to narrow domains, while open-ended self-generation broadens the task space but lacks reliable verification, allowing misleading rewards to pollute the training loop. We identify agent skills as a powerful middle ground to reconcile this tension: each skill ensures deep, verifiable execution in a specific scenario, while dynamic routing across skills maintains open-ended task variety. Leveraging this insight, we introduce Skill Self-Play (Skill-SP), a co-evolutionary framework comprising a proposer, a solver, and a dynamic skill controller. Orchestrated via a reinforcement learning loop, these components co-evolve in a continuous self-play loop: the proposer generates challenging tasks conditioned on dynamically sampled skills; the solver explores candidate solutions to push its capability boundaries; and the skill controller collects execution feedback to update and expand the skill library. This interactive co-evolution effectively bridges the gap between structured verification and open-ended exploration. Empirical evaluations on tool-use and reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that Skill-SP, serving as a robust evolution engine, consistently pushes the performance ceiling of competent backbones while catalyzing striking turnarounds for initially misaligned models. Our code is available at https://github.com/Qwen-Applications/skill-self-play.

</details>

### [30] [Cloud-Native Evaluation-as-a-Service: A Microservices Architecture for Scalable AI Monitoring with Conformal Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21623)
*Lei Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了EaaS云原生参考架构，将AI评估方法以六个无状态Kubernetes微服务形式实现，包括共形预测、校准评估、漂移检测、公平性监控、DAG管道编排和结果存储API，并通过实验验证了方法学有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏一个将共形预测即服务、微服务解耦和DAG编排相结合的平台来系统化AI评估方法的运维部署。

Method: 设计了EaaS架构，包含六个微服务：有限样本校正的自适应预测集共形预测、校准评估、RFF-MMD漂移检测、Bootstrap置信区间公平性监控、DAG管道编排器和结果存储API，均在Kubernetes上部署。

Result: 共形预测覆盖率在1.4个百分点内达到名义目标；10%模拟插补对覆盖率影响小于1.5%；RFF-MMD在中等启发式带宽下检测功率100%，I类错误5-8.5%；公平性监控发现种族间DP差距0.33；共形预测和校准服务在批大小100时p99延迟低于2ms。

Conclusion: EaaS是目前唯一将共形预测即服务、微服务解耦和DAG编排三者结合的AI评估平台，可高效支持生产环境中的模型可靠性监控。

Abstract: We present EaaS, a cloud-native reference architecture that operationalizes AI evaluation methods as six stateless Kubernetes microservices: conformal prediction with finite-sample-corrected Adaptive Prediction Sets, calibration assessment, drift detection via RFF-approximated Maximum Mean Discrepancy, fairness monitoring with bootstrap confidence intervals, a DAG-based pipeline orchestrator, and a result storage API. We validate four key methodological concerns. First, empirical coverage is consistent with the marginal conformal guarantee across K=50 random calibration/test splits, with mean coverage within 1.4 percentage points of the nominal target. Second, all four MMLU answer tokens appear in the top-20 logprobs with 0% imputation needed, and simulated imputation at 10% produces less than 1.5% coverage impact. Third, RFF-MMD achieves 100% detection power for mild and severe drift at the median heuristic bandwidth, with Type I error between 5-8.5%. Fourth, fairness monitoring on the UCI Adult Income dataset reveals significant demographic parity disparities by race (DP gap=0.33) with stable alerts across sequential batches. Conformal prediction and calibration services achieve sub-2ms p99 latency at batch size 100; RFF-MMD requires ~500ms suited for periodic batch monitoring. A comparison with four open-source tools suggests that, to the best of our knowledge, no current platform combines conformal-prediction-as-a-service, microservice decomposition, and DAG-based orchestration.

</details>


### [31] [On the Depth Scalability of Logic Gate Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21633)
*Taegun An,Dohun kim,Haebeom Lee,Changhee Joo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出输入锚定逻辑门网络（IALGN），通过让每个逻辑门同时处理隐藏特征和原始输入，解决了深度逻辑门网络（LGN）中深度增加无法带来性能提升的问题，在多个数据集上实现了超过100层的稳定深度-精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有逻辑门网络（LGN）增加深度无法可靠地带来性能提升，作者识别出两个原因：深度松弛LGN的优化崩溃，以及即使通过偏置初始化和直通估计稳定训练后仍存在的拓扑限制。因此，仅保证可训练性不够，更深层还需要接收支持有效计算的信息。

Method: 提出输入锚定逻辑门网络（IALGN），每个门将演化的隐藏特征与直接输入锚点相结合。这种拓扑结构保留了一个计算主干，同时使每一层都基于原始输入进行条件处理。还引入随机k锚点松弛以在不松弛主干的情况下改进锚点选择。

Result: 在MNIST、CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100上，IALGN在固定宽度下实现了超过100层的一致深度-精度提升，而其他LGN拓扑则饱和或退化。层探针、拓扑消融和有效深度分析表明，输入锚定产生了逐步更具信息量的表示，并保留了更长的计算路径。

Conclusion: 逻辑门网络中实现可扩展深度需要同时满足两个条件：稳定的优化过程和能够支持输入条件化精炼的信息访问模式。

Abstract: Logic Gate Networks (LGNs) implement computation through compositions of Boolean operations, yet unlike classical Boolean circuits, existing LGNs do not reliably benefit from increased depth. We identify two distinct causes: optimization collapse in deep relaxed LGNs and a topology-induced limitation that persists even when skip-biased initialization and straight-through estimation stabilize training. Thus, trainability alone is insufficient; deeper layers must also receive information that supports useful computation.
  We introduce Input-Anchored Logic Gate Networks (IALGNs), in which each gate combines an evolving hidden feature with a direct input anchor. This topology preserves a computational spine while conditioning every layer on the original input. We show that a depth-D path can depend on up to D+1 input bits and establish a strict path-wise depth hierarchy. Random-k anchor relaxation further improves anchor selection without relaxing the spine.
  Across MNIST, CIFAR-10, and CIFAR-100, IALGNs achieve consistent fixed-width depth--accuracy improvements beyond 100 layers, whereas alternative LGN topologies saturate or degrade. Layer-wise probes, topology ablations, and effective-depth analysis show that input anchoring produces progressively more informative representations and preserves longer computational paths. These results demonstrate that scalable depth in LGNs requires both stable optimization and an information-access pattern that supports input-conditioned refinement.

</details>


### [32] [MotifRole-Diff: Risk-Optimal Role-Aware Corruption for Masked Molecular Graph Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21634)
*Tasfia Nuzhat Ornee,Elias Hossain,Ivan Garibay,Niloofar Yousef*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MotifRole-Diff，一种基于角色感知的掩码策略，根据不同分子图令牌角色的去噪难度和结构影响分配掩码率，在保持模型架构不变的情况下，显著提升分子图生成的有效性和FCD指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统均匀掩码策略忽视了分子图中不同令牌角色在去噪难度和结构重要性上的显著差异，导致生成质量受限。

Method: 提出角色感知的掩码调度，根据经验测量的去噪难度和图级扰动影响分配掩码预算，并理论刻画了风险最优分配条件。

Result: 在QM9上有效性从0.905提升至0.944，FCD从1.701降至1.609；在MOSES上有效性从0.920提升至0.938，FCD从2.125降至1.850。

Conclusion: 结构化感知的掩码策略优于均匀调度，为序列化分子图扩散提供了更有效的腐蚀方案。

Abstract: Masked discrete diffusion for molecular graph generation typically applies a uniform corruption schedule to all tokens in a lossless graph-to-sequence representation, implicitly treating structurally heterogeneous molecular components as equally difficult and equally important to reconstruct. However, different molecular graph token roles exhibit substantial variation in denoising difficulty and their influence on the decoded molecule, motivating role-specific corruption strategies. We introduce MotifRole-Diff, a role-aware corruption process that allocates masking rates according to empirically measured denoising difficulty and graph-level perturbation impact while preserving the model architecture, clean sequence space, and lossless molecular-graph decoder. We formulate schedule selection as the risk-optimal allocation of a fixed masking budget across token roles. Our theorem characterizes optimality for the modeled role-weighted residual risk, while downstream generation performance is evaluated empirically. Under matched architecture, training budget, and sampling compute, MotifRole-Diff improves validity on QM9 from 0.905 to 0.944 while reducing FCD from 1.701 to 1.609, and on MOSES improves validity from 0.920 to 0.938 while reducing FCD from 2.125 to 1.850. Role-wise diagnostics further show improved reconstruction across molecular graph token categories. Together, these matched-compute results indicate that structurally informed corruption is a more effective masking strategy than uniform schedules for serialized molecular graph diffusion.

</details>


### [33] [Toward User-Conditioned Evaluation of Personal LLM Agents under Temporal Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21635)
*Pin Qian,Su Wang,Yihang Chen,Qiaolin Yu,Xiaoyuan Wang,Zhitong Guo,Zhicheng Wang,Junxian You*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出现有个人智能体基准测试将工具调用、记忆和安全等能力孤立评估，未能捕捉跨组件的故障传播。作者提出四项必要条件：显式时间干预、跨干预的持久状态、诱导的跨维度效应、用户条件状态变化。在针对性审计中未发现满足全部条件的现有协议，并提出了最小化基准设计及候选报告指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准将个人智能体的工具调用、记忆、策略遵守等能力割裂评估，缺乏统一协议来评测不同用户条件状态下的动态适应性和故障跨组件传播。

Method: 提出四项必要条件（显式时间干预、持久状态、跨维度效应、用户条件状态变化），对公开基准协议进行针对性审计，并提出最小基准设计与候选报告指标。

Result: 审计的公开基准协议中未发现完全满足全部四项条件的协议。

Conclusion: 未来个人智能体评估需要引入用户条件化适应性的基准设计，本文提出的指标可作为该需求的报告工具。

Abstract: Personal agents maintain memories, learned skills, tool configurations, and policy state that evolve with each user. Existing agent benchmarks often evaluate these capabilities in isolation: tool benchmarks test invocation under fixed APIs, memory benchmarks test recall or forgetting, and safety benchmarks test static policy compliance. We argue that personal-agent evaluation requires a different protocol: replaying the same temporal intervention across different persistent user-conditioned states and measuring how failures propagate across agent components. We formalize this requirement as four conditions: explicit temporal intervention, persistent state across the intervention, induced cross-dimensional effects, and variation in user-conditioned state. A focused audit of public benchmark protocols selected by explicit inclusion criteria identifies several close cases. Under our explicitly narrow operationalization, we did not find a protocol in that audited set satisfying all four conditions. This claim is scoped as a focused gap analysis with bounded literature coverage. This position paper proposes a minimal benchmark design and candidate reporting metrics for user-conditioned adaptation. The result is a concrete design requirement for future personal-agent evaluation, with metrics used as reporting tools for that requirement.

</details>


### [34] [Measuring the Dependency Gap: Diagnosing Inter-Column Fidelity in Tabular Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21636)
*Jie Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了合成表格数据评估中常用指标对列间依赖关系感知不足的问题，提出了一种依赖感知的诊断方法（XGB-C2ST），并发现即使先进的流匹配生成器仍存在依赖差距，且简单干预无法解决此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成表格数据的评估指标（如逻辑回归C2ST、成对Trend分数）对列间依赖关系不敏感，导致生成的数据虽然边际分布看似真实，但破坏了关键的结构依赖，尤其影响少数类样本的效用。

Method: 提出一种依赖感知的保真度诊断方法（XGB-C2ST），将判别信号分解为边际、依赖和数值-类别交叉分量，以完全因子化参考（破坏所有依赖）和真实数据为锚点，评估流匹配生成器（TabbyFlow/EF-VFM）的表现。

Result: 标准指标忽略了真实的依赖差距；破坏依赖会彻底降低少数类效用；生成器的残余依赖差距虽小但持续存在；16倍模型容量增加无法缩小差距，说明问题不在容量或结构限制，而在于缺乏直接的依赖监督。

Conclusion: 依赖差距源于缺少直接的依赖监督而非模型容量或结构限制，且无法通过廉价干预（如依赖机制、后验Copula校正或增加容量）解决，这对依赖此类简单修复的领域提出了警示。

Abstract: Synthetic tabular data is valued for preserving not only each column's marginal distribution but the dependencies between columns -- structure that carries much of the discriminative signal for minority classes in imbalanced domains such as fraud and clinical risk. Yet the metrics most commonly used to certify synthetic tabular data are, we show, largely blind to inter-column dependency: a baseline that models every column independently (and therefore destroys all dependency) is judged indistinguishable from real data by the logistic-regression C2ST, and the pairwise Trend score is only partially sensitive. We introduce a dependency-aware fidelity diagnostic that decomposes a strong classifier two-sample test (XGB-C2ST) into marginal, dependency, and numerical-categorical cross components, anchored between a worst-case fully-factorized reference (all dependency destroyed) and a best-case real-data oracle. Applying it to a state-of-the-art flow-matching generator (TabbyFlow/EF-VFM), we find a real dependency gap that standard metrics miss; destroying dependency outright collapses minority-class utility, and the generator's residual gap carries a smaller, consistent utility cost. We then ask whether this gap reflects a structural limitation of mean-field generative objectives. It does not: consistent with recent recovery results for variational flow matching, the objective is asymptotically exact. Yet the gap is stubborn -- a 16x increase in model capacity does not close it -- pointing to the absence of direct dependency supervision rather than a capacity or structural limit. Consistent with this, and because the residual gap is higher-order, no cheap intervention closes it: not an in-model dependency mechanism, not post-hoc copula correction, and not the 16x capacity increase -- a caution for a field that assumes such fixes help.

</details>


### [35] [Quasi-Monte Carlo Initialization for Meta-Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21637)
*Julian G. Soltes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了准蒙特卡洛（QMC）权重初始化在元强化学习基准环境中的效果，发现QMC元先验在相似未见环境中训练收敛优于正交初始化，但在不相似任务中正交初始化表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索准蒙特卡洛（QMC）权重初始化方法在元强化学习中的有效性，以提升训练收敛性能。

Method: 使用多种采样方法限制种群搜索，从基线任务集合中聚合最优先验，并在现代基准环境中比较QMC元先验与正交初始化（SB3默认）的表现。

Result: QMC元先验在相似未见连续控制环境中训练收敛优于正交初始化；在不相似任务中，正交初始化的无偏搜索表现全局更优。

Conclusion: QMC权重初始化在相似任务迁移场景中有效，但正交初始化在任务差异较大时更具鲁棒性。

Abstract: This paper explores the efficacy of quasi-Monte Carlo (QMC) weight initialization for meta-reinforcement learning within modern benchmark environments. Various sampling methods are used to bound a population-based search and aggregate an optimal prior from a baseline set of tasks. The QMC meta-priors show improvements in training convergence compared to modern orthogonal (SB3) defaults when extrapolated to similar unseen continuous control environments. In dissimilar tasks, the orthogonal orientation was globally superior for an unbiased search.

</details>


### [36] [Toward Goal-Agnostic Joint-Embedding Predictive Control of Partial Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21644)
*Jonathan Gallagher,Roberto Guglielmi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于联合嵌入预测架构(JEPA)的偏微分方程目标无关控制框架，使用预训练的小型2D ViT编码器和动作条件潜空间动力学，结合模型预测路径积分(MPPI)控制器。研究发现，在潜空间中使用显式物理可观测量的控制目标优于直接最小化欧氏距离，特别是学习线性动能探针在冻结潜空间滚动预测中能达到R²=0.989，并在2D纳维-斯托克斯基准测试中显著优于潜空间L²规划方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PDE控制方法通常需要针对特定目标设计，缺乏通用性和可迁移性。作者希望构建一种目标无关的控制框架，使得潜空间动力学模型能够在没有奖励或下游目标的情况下离线训练，然后可以被不同的控制目标重复使用，提高模型的泛用性和效率。

Method: 采用联合嵌入预测架构(JEPA)，包含小尺寸2D ViT编码器和动作条件潜空间动力学模型，离线无监督训练后冻结；使用模型预测路径积分(MPPI)控制器进行规划；提出在潜空间中使用线性动能探针(Kinetic-Energy probe)作为控制目标，替代直接最小化潜空间欧氏距离(L²)。

Result: 线性动能探针在冻结潜空间滚动预测中R²=0.989；在2D纳维-斯托克斯基准测试中，动能探针规划将50轮原生奖励从-12.08±0.86提升至-10.90±0.91，速度场RMSE从0.0765降至0.0692；在三种不同非周期目标上，动能规划后期场RMSE比潜空间L²规划降低53%(0.0220 vs 0.0469)，30对试验全部获胜；稳定控制达到2.7%平均相对误差。

Conclusion: 潜空间动力学可以保持动态性和目标无关性，而校准后的可观测量（只要保证唯一延拓性）可能比潜空间本身更适合作为状态控制的目标。虽然线性探针在测量噪声和像素缺失情况下表现脆弱，但整体结果支持了上述主张。

Abstract: We present a goal-agnostic control framework for partial differential equations (PDEs) built around a joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA). The small 2D ViT encoder and action-conditioned latent dynamics are trained offline without a reward or downstream goal, frozen, and reused by a model-predictive path integral (MPPI) controller. We find that when available, the control objective is better applied to an explicit physical observable (provided injectivity) than to minimizing raw Euclidean distance ($L^2$) in the learned latent space. For a learned linear kinetic-energy (KE) probe on frozen latent rollouts we can reproduce held-out trajectories with $R^2=0.989$, while requiring no change to the underlying world model. On the PDE Control Gym 2D Navier--Stokes benchmark, using KE-probe planning improves the matched 50-episode native reward from $-12.08\pm0.86$ for latent-$L^2$ planning to $-10.90\pm0.91$ (95\% CI), while lowering last-quarter velocity-field RMSE from $0.0765$ to $0.0692$. Across three intentionally withheld, dissimilar, aperiodic targets, KE planning lowers late field RMSE by $53\%$ relative to latent-$L^2$ planning ($0.0220$ versus $0.0469$), winning all 30 paired episodes. The same frozen model also supports controls targeting stabilization around a steady configuration via direct regulation of KE achieving $2.7\%$ mean relative error. While the latent probe is brittle to measurement noise and missing pixels, we believe the results support the claim that latent dynamics can remain both dynamic and goal-agnostic while calibrated observables (granted they guarantee unique continuation) may be a better objective for state control

</details>
