Abstract: Multi-horizon latent consistency is a common training knob in video predictors and world models, but practitioners rarely know what it does to transition geometry. We treat lambda, the weight on multi-step latent agreement, as a diagnostic control and measure an empirical expansion proxy L20,q95 together with horizon-20 prediction error E20. On Moving-MNIST (n=6 seeds at the critical pair), raising lambda from 0 to 0.8 cuts L20 from 4.96 +/- 2.01 to 1.01 +/- 0.06 (paired t p=0.005, Wilcoxon p=0.031) and halves E20 (0.365 to 0.177, paired t p=1.1e-13). Four of six seeds cross L<1 at lambda=0.8. The same loss does not produce population L<1 on action-conditioned Pendulum-v1 or CartPole-v1, nor on KTH Actions video, even when E20 improves. An associational mediation analysis on MMNIST gives r-hat=0.94 (95% CI [0.88, 1.00], n=27, B=2000); lambda was not randomized. Defensive checks (architectural baselines, exogenous stress, WorldTest, MPC, scaling) mostly support a narrow claim: soft consistency can push passive video toward a near-contractive band, and that band is domain-limited. A stochastic-forcing law L20 ~ 1.23 + 1.82 eta at lambda=0.8 (bootstrap slope CI [1.73, 1.92], R^2=0.96) unifies control domains on the same curve via calibrated eta_eff. Complete joint slices at lambda in {0.4, 1.2} (30/30 cells, 5 eta x 3 seeds) show comparable linear L20(eta) slopes (~1.69 and ~2.00); we do not fit a continuous (lambda, eta) surface. We do not report DreamerV3 or TD-MPC2 returns.

</details>


### [38] [Adjustment Speed as a Safety Constraint for Nonstationary Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21646)
*Timothy Tomashevskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将调整速度作为非平稳强化学习的安全约束，通过估计适应需求并与智能体的恢复能力进行比较，在预测到适应需求超标时主动收紧动作空间并启用屏蔽机制，以减少环境变化时的安全违规行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全强化学习方法通常假设环境是平稳的，未将适应速度视为安全问题。然而在非平稳环境中，延迟适应可能导致瞬态不安全行为，因此需要一种能主动应对环境变化的安全框架。

Method: 提出适应可行性框架：利用学习到的上下文表示和短时域上下文预测来估计适应需求，与智能体的校准恢复能力进行比较；当预测的适应需求超过恢复能力时，主动收紧允许动作集并激活动作级屏蔽来减少不安全行为。

Result: 在非平稳驾驶环境的实验中，该方法主要减少了与环境变化对齐的短时域窗口内的安全违规。消融研究表明，屏蔽在峰尾风险抑制上更保守，而优化级调整进一步减少了短时域切换条件违规。

Conclusion: 适应可行性可作为非平稳强化学习的实用安全原则，主动干预能在环境变化期间有效提高安全性。

Abstract: Ensuring safety in reinforcement learning under nonstationarity requires determining whether a learning system can safely adapt to forecasted environmental change within the required recovery horizon. Existing safe reinforcement learning methods typically assume stationary environments and do not explicitly consider adaptation speed as a safety concern. However, when environments evolve over time, delayed adaptation may result in transient unsafe behavior.
  This paper proposes adjustment speed as a safety constraint for nonstationary reinforcement learning. The central idea is to define safety in terms of adaptation feasibility: future states or regions may become unsafe when the adaptation required to remain safe exceeds the learning system's calibrated recovery capacity. The proposed framework uses learned context representations and short-horizon context forecasts to estimate adaptation demand and compare it with the agent's achievable adaptation capacity.
  When predicted adaptation demand exceeds the calibrated recovery capacity, the framework proactively tightens the admissible action set and activates an action-level shield to reduce unsafe behavior before violations occur.
  Experiments in a nonstationary driving environment show that the proposed approach primarily reduces safety violations in short-horizon windows aligned with context changes. Ablation studies further show that shielding is more conservative for peak- and tail-risk suppression, while optimization-level adjustment provides additional reductions in short-horizon switch-conditioned violations.
  These results support adaptation feasibility as a practical safety principle for reinforcement learning under nonstationarity and demonstrate that proactive intervention can improve safety during periods of environmental change.

</details>


### [39] [A Drift Stable Quantum Federated Learning for Intelligent Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21647)
*Shanika Iroshi Nanayakkara,Shiva Raj Pokhrel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于深度展开的本地优化的抗漂移量子联邦学习框架DUQFL-Prox，通过自适应展开SPSA更新和近端项约束，在金融欺诈和基因分类任务上提升了稳定性、泛化性和客户端公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子联邦学习中，异构客户端数据和噪声量子优化常导致本地更新不稳定、客户端漂移以及客户端间性能不公平，而现有方法缺乏针对这些问题的有效解决方案。

Method: 提出DUQFL-Prox框架：每个客户端使用自适应展开的SPSA（同时扰动随机逼近）进行本地更新，并引入近端项约束使本地模型接近全局模型；同时设计轻量级控制器学习步骤特定的优化参数，以提升聚合后性能。

Result: 在金融欺诈检测和基因分类任务上，DUQFL-Prox相比标准QFL基线方法，在稳定性、泛化性和客户端公平性方面均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 深度展开的量子联邦学习能够支持异构分布式环境下更可靠、更公平的智能服务。

Abstract: Quantum federated learning enables distributed clients to train quantum neural networks without sharing local data, making it promising for privacy-aware intelligent services. Intelligent services in this context refer to privacy-sensitive distributed decision systems, such as fraud detection and genomic classification, where reliable and fair client-level learning is as important as the accuracy of the aggregate model. However, heterogeneous client data and noisy quantum optimization often cause unstable local updates, client drift, and unfair performance between clients. This paper proposes DUQFL-Prox, a drift-stable quantum federated learning framework based on deep-unfolded local optimization. Instead of using a fixed local optimizer, each client performs adaptive unfolded SPSA updates, while a proximal term keeps the local model close to the global model. A lightweight controller learns step-specific optimization parameters to improve post-aggregation performance. Experiments on financial fraud and genomic classification tasks show that DUQFL-Prox improves stability, generalization, and client fairness compared with standard QFL baselines. The results suggest that deep-unfolded quantum federated learning can support more reliable and fair intelligent services in heterogeneous distributed environments.

</details>


### [40] [Shallower ReLU Network Representations via Exact Linear Algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21651)
*Kilian Rueß,Gennadiy Averkov,Florestan Brunck,Moritz Grillo,Christoph Hertrich,Georg Loho,Jack Stade,Moritz Stargalla,Matthew Sun,Martin Winter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了：对于 n ≤ 10，n个实数的最大值函数可通过两层隐藏层的ReLU网络精确表示；对于 n > 10，则需 ⌈log₅(n/2)⌉+1 层隐藏层。通过广义铰链超平面表示，该深度界适用于 ℝᵈ 上的所有连续分段线性函数。结果优于 Bakaev 等人（STOC'26）的先前结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索ReLU网络对最大值函数的精确表示能力，特别是需要多少隐藏层才能实现精确表达，这是理解神经网络表达能力的基础问题之一。

Method: 将问题归约为精确有理线性代数：通过对称性约简将必要抵消编码为有限有理线性系统，并计算求解。对于n>10，利用max₁₀第一隐藏层仅包含成对最大值的结构特性，递归代入更大网络。

Result: 1) n≤10时，max_n可用两层隐藏层ReLU网络精确表示；2) n>10时，max_n可用 ⌈log₅(n/2)⌉+1 层隐藏层表示（< log₅(n)+1.5694）；3) d≤9时，ℝᵈ上任何连续分段线性函数均有两层隐藏层ReLU表示。

Conclusion: 改进了 Bakaev 等人（STOC'26）的结果（该工作仅实现max₅的两层表示，且上界为 ⌈log₃(n-2)⌉+1），将两层表示能力从n=5扩展到n=10，并降低了深度上界的底数从3到5。

Abstract: We prove that the maximum of $n$ real numbers is exactly representable by a ReLU network with two hidden layers for every $n\le 10$. The constructions are obtained by reducing the problem to exact rational linear algebra: after a symmetry reduction, the necessary cancellations are encoded in finite linear systems over $\mathbb{Q}$, which we solve and verify computationally. The representation of $\max_{10}$ has a structured first hidden layer consisting only of pairwise maxima, a feature that allows it to be recursively substituted into larger networks. We use this to show that for every $n>10$, the maximum $\max_{n}$ can be exactly represented with $\lceil{\log_5 (n / 2)\rceil}+1 < \log_5(n) +1.5694$ hidden layers. Via the generalized hinging-hyperplane representation [Wang, Sun, IEEE Trans. Inf. Theory 2005], the same depth bound holds for all continuous piecewise-linear functions on $\mathbb{R}^d$, with $d+1$ in place of $n$. In particular, every continuous piecewise-linear function on $\mathbb{R}^d$ for $d\le 9$ admits a two-hidden-layer ReLU representation. Our results improve on [Bakaev, Brunck, Hertrich, Stade, Yehudayoff, STOC'26]. In that work, the authors established a two-hidden-layer representation for $\max_{5}$ and an upper bound of $\lceil{\log_3 (n-2)\rceil}+1$ hidden layers for $\max_{n}$.

</details>


### [41] [Molt: A Scalable PyTorch-Native Training Framework for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21653)
*Jian Hu,Huiying Li,Hao Zhang,Binfeng Xu,Yifan Zhang,Shaokun Zhang,Hemil Desai,Michael Demoret,Pavlo Molchanov,Jan Kautz,Yi Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Molt是一个轻量级、PyTorch原生的强化学习训练框架，代码简洁紧凑，支持异步训练、多模态和混合专家策略，性能与基于Megatron的SOTA方案相当，已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习框架中，每次算法修改（新估计器、流水线阶段、rollout方案等）都需要改动训练器、分布式后端和rollout胶水代码多个层面，研究者迭代成本高昂。Molt旨在降低这一成本。

Method: Molt采用PyTorch原生架构，保持代码库紧凑简洁，使研究者能全局理解；使用异步循环训练多模态和混合专家策略，确保只训练模型自身生成的token，保持token、策略版本和模型语义的一致性。

Result: 在匹配的完全异步协议下，Molt的性能在统计上与基于Megatron的SOTA方案相当。

Conclusion: 轻量化设计不会牺牲性能，Molt是一个高效、可理解的强化学习训练框架，已开源并提供食谱和容器。

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning research is constant algorithm modification, new estimators, new pipeline stages, new rollout schemes, and in mainstream frameworks each change threads through layers of trainer, distributed backend, and rollout glue: the cost lands on the researcher at every iteration. Molt is a PyTorch-native training framework built to keep that cost small: a codebase compact and clean enough for a researcher to hold in their head, and for an AI coding assistant to read and reason about in its entirety, so the algorithm flow can be traced and changed end to end. The agent is an ordinary program, and one asynchronous loop trains multimodal and mixture-of-experts policies while never training on a token it did not generate, consistent in tokens, policy versions, and model semantics. Leanness does not cost performance: under a matched, fully asynchronous protocol, Molt is statistically comparable to a state-of-the-art Megatron-based stack. Molt is open source and provides recipes and containers at https://github.com/NVIDIA-NeMo/labs-molt.

</details>


### [42] [Physically Constrained Federated Additive Models for O-RAN SLA-Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21665)
*Aubida A. Al-Hameed,Mohammed M. H. Qazzaz,Maryam Hafeez,Syed A. Zaidi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Monotone FedNAM，一种物理约束的联邦可加模型，用于O-RAN中跨基站的SLA违规预测，在保持可审计性的同时消除违反无线物理规律的预测问题，并降低65%上行流量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: O-RAN中主动服务保障需预测切片SLA违规，但预测模型需可审计且不能聚合跨基站的每切片KPI（商业敏感）。现有神经可加模型（NAM）虽可审计，但无约束的NAM会学习到违反无线物理规律的效果，如信道质量变好却预测风险更高。

Method: 提出Monotone FedNAM，一种联邦可加模型，将具有明确物理方向的KPI表示为单调样条，其约束在FedAvg聚合后依然保持；对有争议的KPI保持无约束。模型可作为Non-RT RIC rApp训练，作为Near-RT RIC xApp部署。

Result: 消除了所有单调性违反，将约束形状一致性从0.71提升至1.00，可泛化到未见过的调度策略，减少65%上行流量，AUC仅下降0.04-0.07。

Conclusion: 物理约束的联邦可加模型能够支持多租户O-RAN服务保障中可审计的SLA风险推理，在保持模型可解释性的同时保证物理合理性。

Abstract: Proactive service assurance in O-RAN requires predicting per-slice SLA violations before they occur. The prediction model must be auditable by operators and must train across base stations without pooling per-slice KPIs, which are commercially sensitive because slices are leased to individual tenants. Neural additive models (NAMs) offer auditability because each KPI contributes through a visible shape function. However, visibility alone does not guarantee physical validity. On the ColO-RAN testbed dataset, unconstrained NAMs learn effects that contradict wireless physics, for example predicting higher risk when channel quality improves. This failure appears under both local and centralized training, and non-IID federated averaging worsens it. We present Monotone FedNAM, a federated additive model in which KPIs with unambiguous physical direction are represented as monotone splines whose constraints survive FedAvg aggregation by construction, while contestable KPIs remain unconstrained. The model trains and operates as a Non-RT RIC rApp and is compact enough for deployment as a Near-RT RIC xApp. Monotone FedNAM eliminates all monotonicity violations, raises constrained shape consistency from 0.71 to 1.00, generalizes to an unseen scheduling policy, and reduces uplink traffic by 65%, at a cost of 0.04 to 0.07 AUC. These results show that physically constrained federated additive models can support auditable SLA risk inference for multi-tenant O-RAN service assurance

</details>


### [43] [Neural Feature Governance: Extending Atom Prevalence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21671)
*Idris Karel Seunda Ekwe,Patrick Tenga Shako,Ernest Parfait Fokoué*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出神经原子盛行度（NAP），一个基于贝叶斯框架的前馈神经网络结构化节点级模型选择方法，通过四阶段流水线实现稀疏、稳定、可解释且准确的模型，在MNIST上仅保留8%的活跃节点，且不确定性分解中模型无知仅占3-4%的预测方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习中的神经网络参数规模庞大，存在透明度低、计算效率差和不确定性量化不可靠等问题，亟需一种同时实现稀疏性、准确性、可解释性和不确定性量化的方法。

Method: NAP是一个四阶段流水线：1) 通过迭代幅度剪枝识别贝叶斯彩票（BLT）；2) 对Spike and Slab独立高斯模型进行软变分训练；3) 基于泊松-二项分布进行最优层大小选择；4) 贝叶斯微调，最终得到稀疏稳定的模型。

Result: 在模拟非线性回归、UCI基准数据集（Concrete, YearPredictionMSD）和MNIST图像分类任务上，NAP在MNIST上将活跃节点降至原始密集架构的8%，实现了最先进的结构稀疏性；不确定性分解显示模型无知仅占总预测方差的3-4%；回归可靠性图显示预测区间覆盖率达93.4%（目标95%）。

Conclusion: NAP是一个可靠、理论扎实且计算可行的解决方案，能够同时实现贝叶斯神经网络的稀疏性、准确性、可解释性和不确定性量化。

Abstract: Neural network compression and interpretability remain open challenges in modern deep learn- ing, where billion-parameter architectures deliver impressive accuracy at the cost of trans- parency, computational efficiency, and reliable uncertainty quantification. This paper introduces Neural Atom Prevalence (NAP), a principled Bayesian framework for structured node-level model selection in feedforward neural networks. NAP introduces the neural atom (activation unit) and functions as a hybrid method operating through a four-phase pipeline: Bayesian Lottery Ticket (BLT) identification via Iterative Magnitude Pruning (IMP), soft variational training of the Spike and Slab Independent Gaussian (SS-IG) model, Poisson-Binomial (PB) optimal layer-size selection, and Bayesian fine-tuning to produce a sparse, stable, interpretable, and accurate model. Extensive empirical validation across simulated nonlinear regression, two UCI benchmark datasets (Concrete, YearPredictionMSD), and the MNIST image classification task demonstrates that NAP achieves state-of-the-art structural sparsity, reducing active nodes to as few as 8% of the original dense architecture on MNIST, while well-calibrated probabilisti- cally: the aleatoric-epistemic uncertainty decomposition reveals that model ignorance accounts for only 3 to 4% of total predictive variance across all experiments, and regression reliability diagrams confirm a near-nominal predictive interval coverage (93.4% observed against a 95% target). These results establish NAP as a reliable, theoretically grounded, and computation- ally tractable solution to the simultaneous pursuit of sparsity, accuracy, interpretability, and uncertainty quantification in Bayesian neural networks.

</details>


### [44] [Self-Poisoning in Adaptive Out-of-Distribution Detection: A Sharp-Threshold Theory and Certified Label-Free Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21673)
*Vishnu Bindu Balachandran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了测试时自适应OOD检测器的动态行为，证明其服从一个可证明的动力学定律，并提出了一个认证准入门控机制来防止检测器崩溃，同时给出了无标签自适应OOD检测的完整可能性/不可能性刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的测试时自适应OOD检测器使用未标记数据流更新记忆库，但其动态行为缺乏理论分析，尤其是不纯度的传播和检测器崩溃的临界条件尚不明确。

Method: 将记忆库不纯度建模为广义Pólya瓮模型，证明几乎必然收敛到均场均衡；提出认证准入门控机制（仅读取冻结储备）切断反馈回路；针对静态校准漂移提出CDC方法。

Result: 证实准入核为仿射函数（R²≥0.996），斜率略低于1（协议签名），检测器近临界设计；在96个设置下预测阈值与经验崩溃匹配，无门控字典AUROC下降达0.163；认证门控在所有污染率下消除相变，CDC恢复标称FPR。

Conclusion: 证明了一个两世界不可能性定理：无标签条件下漂移和污染不可区分，存在封闭形式的功率上限；所提方法逼近该上限，给出了完整的可能性/不可能性刻画。

Abstract: Test-time adaptive out-of-distribution (OOD) detectors update a memory bank from the unlabelled stream. We show this adaptation obeys a provable dynamical law. Modelling bank impurity as a generalized Pólya urn, we prove almost-sure convergence to a mean-field equilibrium whose slope acts as a reproduction number. Below one, impurity stays benign. Above one, the bank is fully poisoned and the detector collapses. The measured admission kernel is affine ($R^2 \ge 0.996$) with slope just below one in every encoder family (a protocol signature), so this detector class is near-critical by design, and across 96 settings the predicted threshold matches the empirical collapse, where ungated dictionaries lose up to $0.163$ AUROC. We then prove that a certified admission gate, reading only a frozen reserve, severs the feedback loop and removes the transition at every contamination rate, even adversarially, while controlling false positives label-free. For the complementary static-calibration failure under drift we give CDC, which restores nominal FPR label-free on all tested drift-affected cells. Finally we prove a two-world impossibility theorem. Drift and contamination are indistinguishable without labels, forcing a closed-form power ceiling our procedure approaches. Together these give a complete possibility/impossibility characterization of label-free adaptive OOD detection.

</details>

*Takato Yasuno*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套三组件框架，利用大语言模型从诊断手册中提取因果三元组，通过检索增强上下文将隐性领域知识显式化，并系统比较了LoRA、QLoRA和QA-LoRA三种微调方法，证明QLoRA在保持87.07%测试准确率的同时，实现了11%的推理加速、72%的GPU内存节省和更强的泛化能力，可用于边缘设备上的自主桥梁诊断代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 桥梁基础设施的退化根因（盐分侵蚀、冰冻、疲劳开裂等）肉眼不可见，专家诊断依赖长期积累的隐性知识。现有方法难以自动化这种潜在的因果推理过程，因此需要一种能将隐性领域知识显式化并实现高效自动化诊断的方法。

Method: 方法包含三个组件：(i)知识三元组提取——使用大语言模型从15-35份诊断PDF手册中提取(损伤→原因)因果三元组，并索引到FAISS向量库中；(ii)检索增强上下文——在训练和推理时检索相关因果三元组与损伤描述拼接，将隐性知识转为显式的编码器上下文；(iii)系统微调比较——在固定Golden Testset上严格比较LoRA、QLoRA和QA-LoRA三种方法。

Result: QLoRA取得与全精度LoRA相同的测试准确率（87.07%），但推理速度提升11%，GPU内存降低72%。在涵盖全部10类损伤原因的100样本多样评估集上，QLoRA比LoRA高出13个百分点。同时引入了分层、去重、带难度标签的Golden Testset作为可复用基准。

Conclusion: QLoRA实现了内存效率与高精度之间的最优权衡，使得在消费级硬件上部署高精度桥梁诊断代理成为可能，为边缘部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Bridge infrastructure deteriorates gradually, yet its root causes---salt intrusion, freezing, fatigue cracking, and others---remain invisible to the naked eye. Expert diagnosis relies on tacit knowledge built over years of practice. We address the challenge of automating this latent causal reasoning by proposing a Damage Cause Encoder that classifies 10-class damage causes from visible damage descriptions $S_i$ for use in autonomous bridge diagnostic agents. Our approach chains three components: (i)Knowledge Triple Extraction---a large language model extracts causal triples of the form (damage $\xrightarrow{\mathtt{caused\_by}}$ cause) from 15--35 diagnostic PDF manuals and indexes them in a FAISS vector store; (ii)Retrieval-Augmented Context---at training and inference time, relevant causal triples $\mathcal{C}_i$ are retrieved and concatenated with $S_i$, converting implicit domain knowledge into explicit Encoder context; (iii)Systematic Fine-tuning Comparison---we conduct a rigorous comparison of LoRA, QLoRA, and QA-LoRA on a fixed Golden Testset (116 stratified samples), demonstrating that QLoRA achieves the optimal trade-off: identical test accuracy (87.07%) to full-precision LoRA, 11% faster inference, 72% lower GPU memory, and superior generalization across diverse unseen inputs. A controlled Golden Testset---stratified, deduplicated, and difficulty-tagged---is introduced as a reusable benchmark contribution. QLoRA further outperforms LoRA by 13 percentage points on a 100-sample diverse evaluation spanning all 10 damage cause classes.These findings enable memory-efficient, high-accuracy diagnostic agents on consumer-grade hardware for edge deployment.

</details>

*Awsaf Tausif Adib,Md. Shahria Sarker Shuvo,Md. Estehaar Ahmed Emon,Mustafa Kamal,Fuad Rahman,Shafin Rahman,Nabeel Mohammed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CARNet提出了一种结合全局周期信息的核心聚合与再分配框架，通过多头核心聚合高效建模跨变量依赖关系，在保持线性复杂度的同时优于多种强基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法中，基于注意力的机制计算复杂度高（二次复杂度），而免注意力模型虽具有线性复杂度但未能有效利用数据中的全局周期性结构，导致在强周期模式下跨变量依赖建模不足。

Abstract: Accurately modeling cross-variate dependencies remains a key challenge in multivariate time series forecasting, particularly in the presence of strong periodic patterns. Many existing approaches rely on attention-based mechanisms that incur quadratic complexity and scale poorly with increasing numbers of variates. Recent attention-free aggregation models address this issue through linear-complexity core-based interactions, but they do not explicitly leverage the global periodic structure present in the data. To overcome this limitation, we propose CARNet, a Cycle-Conditioned Core Aggregation and Redistribution framework that integrates global recurrent cycle information into efficient core based interaction modeling via Multihead Core Aggregation. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world multivariate forecasting benchmarks demonstrate that CARNet consistently outperforms strong transformer and non-attention baselines across diverse prediction horizons while preserving linear-complexity modeling of cross-variate dependencies.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏注意力方法通过蒸馏密集教师模型的注意力模式来训练选择器，但前提假设——注意力能够揭示模型实际使用的上下文——缺乏验证。作者希望检验这一假设是否成立。

Method: 通过在检索任务中掩码上下文的不同部分，并测量答案是否改变，来区分“注意力”与“因果依赖”。比较基于注意力和基于因果依赖训练的选择器性能。

Result: 注意力与因果依赖经常不一致；基于注意力蒸馏的选择器继承这种不一致。在两步引用任务上，基于注意力的选择器仅41%准确率，基于因果证据的达99%。预训练模型中也存在同样问题。

Conclusion: 注意力不等于因果依赖；基于因果依赖的证据（可通过掩码自动获取）作为训练目标，效果匹配或优于基于注意力的蒸馏。

Abstract: Sparse attention reduces the cost of long contexts by allowing each query to read only selected parts of the input. These selectors are often trained by distilling the attention patterns of a dense teacher, assuming that attention reveals which context the teacher actually uses. We test that assumption on retrieval tasks where the evidence for each answer is known exactly. By masking parts of the context and measuring whether the answer changes, we find that attention and causal dependence often disagree, and distilled selectors inherit the mismatch. Teachers attend to outdated facts they have learned to ignore, and their attention can vary across training runs even when they rely on the same evidence. In a two-step reference task, attention at the answer skips the intermediate step because it was resolved earlier in the forward pass: a selector trained on attention achieves 41% accuracy, while the same selector trained on causal evidence reaches 99% and matches the teacher. These evidence sets require no annotation: recovered from a frozen teacher by masking alone, they train selectors to the same accuracy. We find the same conflict in pretrained models: Qwen2.5-3B gives more attention to an outdated fact than the current one on 58% of conflicting-fact examples despite answering correctly, while Gemma-2-9B rises from 56% to 99% accuracy when restricted to the two relevant sentences. Attention shows where a model looks, not necessarily what its answer depends on; across the regimes we tested, that dependence matched or outperformed attention as a training target.

</details>

*Maximilian Dax,Theo Heimel,Gilles Louppe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于机器学习的模拟推理（SBI）在科学工程逆问题中的应用，涵盖贝叶斯与频率统计框架，介绍了神经后验估计、神经似然估计等方法在参数估计、经验贝叶斯及解褶积任务中的应用，并讨论了验证方法与局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器学习的发展，模拟推理（SBI）在解决科学和工程中的逆问题（如参数推断和探测器效应反演）中变得越来越重要，需要系统梳理其统计框架和方法的适用性。

Method: 综述了贝叶斯和频率统计两种框架，介绍了基于机器学习的SBI方法，包括神经后验估计和神经似然估计，并展示了这些方法在参数估计、经验贝叶斯以及解褶积（unfolding）任务中的应用。

Result: 说明了SBI方法能够应用于多种统计推断任务，并强调了验证推断结果的重要性，同时指出了SBI在机器学习应用中的局限性。

Conclusion: 基于机器学习的SBI是解决逆问题的有力工具，但需要谨慎验证结果并注意其方法局限。

Abstract: Simulation-based inference (SBI) with machine learning is an increasingly important tool for solving inverse problems in science and engineering, including parameter inference and the inversion of detector effects. We provide an overview of the Bayesian and frequentist statistical frameworks, describe how machine-learning-based SBI methods, such as neural posterior estimation and neural likelihood estimation, can be used for parameter estimation within these frameworks, and show that the same methods can also be applied to Empirical Bayes or unfolding tasks. We also discuss how to validate inference results and the limitations of SBI with machine learning.

</details>


### [49] [A Defense of the Quadratic Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21716)
*Alexandru Meterez,Pranav Ajit Nair,Depen Morwani,Cengiz Pehlevan,Sham Kakade,Alex Damian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过将神经网络优化简化为二次模型，在150M参数、3B token的LLM训练中验证了该模型对未来10%训练窗口内动态的预测能力，并分析了Hessian谱与局部稳定性，发现其结构与batch size、预条件器等密切相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有优化理论依赖理想化模型，但模型的可解性与描述真实优化动态的准确性之间存在权衡。本文旨在检验最简单的二次模型在LLM训练中的预测能力，并探索其适用边界。

Method: 在LLM训练中间检查点进行泰勒展开构建局部二次模型，预测后续训练动态；使用Lanczos求积法深入估计Hessian谱的尾部结构；通过局部线性稳定性分析比较理论与实际。

Result: 二次模型能准确预测长达10%训练窗口的优化动态；Hessian谱在特征值和特征向量上均展现出丰富的结构，且依赖于batch size、预条件器和训练时间；LLM优化通常处于随机的边缘稳定性，其性质由batch size决定。

Conclusion: 二次模型可以作为预训练优化动态的一个理论上可处理的代理模型，为理解和分析LLM优化行为提供了新的视角和工具。

Abstract: Due to the complexity of neural network loss landscapes, optimization theory is forced to rely on idealized models, and there is generally a tradeoff between how theoretically tractable the model is, and how accurately it describes the true optimization dynamics. In this work, we stress test the simplest possible model of optimization -- the quadratic model -- and show that it can be surprisingly predictive in an LLM setting with 150M parameters and 3B training tokens. Specifically, we show that Taylor expanding the model and the loss function at intermediate checkpoints through training can accurately predict the optimization dynamics over windows that can last up to 10\% of training. Having established this agreement, we then turn to analyzing the structure of these local quadratic optimization problems through two lenses: the Hessian spectrum and local stability. Using Lanczos quadrature with extremely deep probes, we are able to estimate the Hessian spectrum deep into the tail, and we find a surprising amount of structure in both the eigenvalues and eigenvectors, which depends on the batch size, preconditioner, and training time. We also empirically test local linear stability at intermediate checkpoints and compare it to theoretical predictions to demonstrate that optimization in LLMs typically occurs at a stochastic edge of stability, whose nature is also determined by batch size. Our results indicate the quadratic model may be a theoretically tractable proxy for pretraining optimization dynamics.

</details>


### [50] [RED-PIM: Reducing Data Movement for Transformers using Processing-in-Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21731)
*Zahra Yousefijamarani,Alaa Alameldeen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RED-PIM提出了一种算法-架构协同设计方案，通过将跨存储体数据移动从O(N²)减少到O(N)，并将中间注意力矩阵从N×N压缩为d×d，显著降低Transformer推理延迟，相比基线PIM实现平均加速66.42%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer模型广泛应用于多个领域，但注意力操作中处理单元与内存之间的数据移动严重限制了其效率。现有基于PIM的Transformer实现受限于昂贵的跨存储体通信和存储体容量有限导致的扩展性问题，PIM的潜力未能充分发挥。

Method: RED-PIM采用算法-架构协同设计：1) 重组矩阵运算以实现局部计算；2) 将中间注意力矩阵从N×N缩小为d×d；3) 优化数据传输策略，将跨存储体数据移动从O(N²)降至O(N)。

Result: 相比基线PIM实现，推理时间降低16.05%至99.99%（几何平均66.42%），长序列增益最大。在真实数据集上，长文档性能提升99.60%，短文档提升13.44%，同时保持或提升了模型精度。

Conclusion: RED-PIM通过减少跨存储体通信和压缩中间矩阵，有效解决了PIM-based Transformer的扩展性问题，实现了可扩展且高效的Transformer推理。

Abstract: Transformers are widely used across many domains, including natural language processing, computer vision, web search, and DNA sequence analysis. Given their broad applicability, improving the performance of transformer models is critical. However, the high volume of data movement between processing units and memory during attention operations significantly limits their efficiency. Processing-In-Memory (PIM) mitigates this issue by performing computations directly inside memory. While prior work has proposed PIM-based transformer implementations, they suffer from costly inter-bank communication, and struggle to scale due to the limited capacity of memory banks. As a result, attention-related data must be split across banks, diminishing the potential benefits of PIM.
  In this work, we propose RED-PIM, an algorithm-architecture co-design that reduces attention latency by minimizing inter-bank data movement from O(N^2) to O(N) and shrinking intermediate attention matrices from N x N to d x d. By reorganizing matrix operations, performing computations locally, and employing an optimized data transfer strategy, RED-PIM significantly reduces computation cost and interconnect traffic. Compared to baseline PIM implementation, RED-PIM achieves inference time reductions ranging from 16.05% to 99.99% (geometric mean of 66.42%), with the largest gains on longer sequences. On real-world datasets, RED-PIM improves performance by 99.60% for long documents and 13.44% for shorter ones, while maintaining or improving accuracy. These results demonstrate RED-PIM's effectiveness for scalable and efficient transformer inference.

</details>


### [51] [Parameter-free Adaptive Sparse Attention via Compression-Based Content Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21752)
*Debarshi Kundu,Swaroop Ghosh,Vasant Honavar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需额外参数的基于gzip压缩比的自适应稀疏注意力机制，在长序列语言建模中性能优于密集注意力和现有稀疏注意力方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应稀疏注意力方法虽优于固定模式，但需要额外可学习参数、自定义梯度估计器或专用CUDA内核，计算开销大且实现复杂。作者探索能否用经典数据压缩提供有效的注意力掩码信号，无需任何额外参数。

Method: 计算每个块的gzip压缩比，识别不可压缩（即信息密度高）的块作为长距离注意力的目标。压缩比是输入依赖的，因此生成的稀疏掩码可自适应动态调整，无需学习参数、辅助损失或自定义内核。

Result: 在PG-19数据集、92M参数、8K上下文中，方法取得1.71 BPB，优于密集注意力(2.89)、BigBird(2.34)、Longformer(3.21)和SBM-Transformer(3.38)；序列越长优势越大，8K时比BigBird好0.63 BPB，收敛速度快3.3倍。

Conclusion: 经典数据压缩（gzip）可作为一种高效、无需参数的自适应稀疏注意力信号，在长序列建模中显著优于现有方法且实现简单。

Abstract: Data-adaptive sparse attention masks substantially outperform fixed patterns (e.g., BigBird and Longformer) and can even exceed dense attention on long sequences. Existing adaptive approaches---including SBM-Transformer, Dynamic Mask Attention, and NSA---typically require additional learnable parameters, custom gradient estimators, or specialized CUDA kernels.
  We show that classical data compression provides an effective masking signal with \textbf{no additional parameters}. By computing per-block gzip compression ratios, we identify non-redundant content blocks and route long-range attention selectively through them. Intuitively, blocks that gzip cannot compress contain information not predictable from local repetition, making them natural long-range attention targets. Because the compression profile is input-dependent, the resulting sparse mask adapts dynamically to content without learned parameters, auxiliary losses, or custom kernels.
  On PG-19 byte-level language modeling at 92M parameters with 8K context, our method achieves 1.71 bits-per-byte (BPB), outperforming dense attention (2.89), BigBird (2.34), Longformer (3.21), and a reimplemented SBM-Transformer (3.38)---the only learned-mask baseline---by up to 1.67 BPB while adding no parameters. The advantage grows with sequence length, with the gap over BigBird widening from 0.05 BPB at 4K context to 0.63 BPB at 8K, while convergence is 3.3$\times$ faster.

</details>


### [52] [Smart predict-then-robustly-optimize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21773)
*Aakil Caunhye,Xuefei Lu,Belen Martin-Barragan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种鲁棒的"预测-然后优化"方法，通过在预测-优化流程中整合鲁棒优化原理，应对协变量特征空间中的扰动，并建立了计算可处理的凸替代函数，理论上证明了其近似误差概率呈次高斯指数衰减，且具有Fisher一致性，实验表明该方法在样本外表现和训练稳定性上均优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统智能预测-优化方法假设特征信息完全准确，但实际中数据特征常受噪声或扰动影响，导致决策策略脆弱。本文旨在弥合这一差距，提出鲁棒性更强的预测-优化框架。

Method: 通过将鲁棒优化原理整合到预测-优化流程中，设计智能预测-鲁棒优化损失函数，并建立计算可处理的凸替代函数，以对抗最坏情况下的特征扰动。

Result: 理论上证明了替代函数的近似误差概率呈次高斯指数衰减，且在温和假设下具有高概率的Fisher一致性；实验表明该鲁棒框架在样本外表现和训练稳定性上显著优于标准方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的鲁棒预测-优化框架在特征扰动环境下比传统方法更优，且即使标准方法配备正则化上游预测，该框架仍能保持优越性。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose and study a robust variant of the smart predict-then-optimize approach that accounts for prediction shifts due to disturbance in the covariate feature space. While traditional integrated-learning-and-optimization models assume that side information is perfectly revealed, empirical data-driven features are frequently corrupted or noisy at the time of decision-making, leading to fragile operational policies. To bridge this gap, we integrate principles of robust optimization directly into the predictive-prescriptive pipeline via a smart predict-then-robustly optimize loss and establish a computationally tractable convex surrogate, designed to hedge against worst-case feature perturbations. On the theoretical front, we formalize the structural validity of this surrogate by proving its approximation error probability decays exponentially according to a sub-Gaussian concentration profile. Furthermore, we establish that under mild assumptions, the surrogate is Fisher consistent with high probability. We also prove necessary conditions under which our framework outperforms standard smart predict-then-optimize and maintain its superiority even when the standard method is equipped with regularized upstream predictions. Numerical experiments validate that our robust framework consistently yields significant performance improvements over standard methods, both in out-of-sample terms and in training stability.

</details>


### [53] [Physiological Signals as a Forensic Modality for Talking-Face Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21776)
*Othmane Harraq,Tamer Aldwairi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于rPPG生理信号的说话人脸深伪检测框架，利用RhythmFormer提取波形并用1D ResNet分类，在Celeb-DF++的TF子集上AUC达0.806，发现不同生成器的检测难度差异源于其生理特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像检测器对说话人脸深伪（TF deepfake）几乎无效，而TF合成没有源真实视频，因此rPPG生理信号成为极具潜力的检测模态。

Method: 使用RhythmFormer提取每段视频的rPPG波形，训练轻量级分类器（如1D ResNet）区分真实与合成生理信号。

Result: 在Celeb-DF++的TF子集上，严格主体独立协议下1D ResNet AUC 0.806、EER 27.8%；复现DeepFakesON-Phys发现其从0.999（旧数据）降至0.622（TF子集）；7个TF生成器的AUC从0.985到0.690不等，排名跨协议完全稳定。

Conclusion: 检测难度差异由各生成器可解释的生理特性决定而非评估噪声，这是本文的主要理论贡献。

Abstract: Talking-face (TF) deepfake generation synthesizes photore- alistic facial video from a static source image and an au- dio signal, producing forgeries that current image-based detectors consistently fail to identify. Unlike face-swap ma- nipulation, TF synthesis has no underlying real video from which to inherit physiological characteristics, making re- mote photoplethysmography (rPPG) a uniquely motivated detection modality for this forgery category. We propose a detection framework that extracts per-video rPPG wave- forms via RhythmFormer and trains a suite of lightweight classifiers to distinguish real from synthesized physiologi- cal signals. Evaluated on the TF subset of Celeb-DF++ un- der a strict subject-independent protocol, where test identi- ties are completely separated from training identities, our 1D ResNet achieves an AUC of 0.806 and EER of 27.8%, placing it within 2.4 points of the best published general- purpose detector (Effort, ICML 2025) while operating ex- clusively on the physiological channel. We document a con- trolled reproduction study of DeepFakesON-Phys, the rep- resentative prior rPPG detector, demonstrating degrada- tion from AUC 0.999 on legacy face-swap data to 0.622 on the TF subset of Celeb-DF++. We further show that detec- tion difficulty is strongly method-dependent: AUC ranges from 0.985 (Real3DPortrait) to 0.690 (IP-LAP) across the seven TF generators, with the ranking remaining perfectly stable across all evaluation protocols. This spread reflects an interpretable physiological property of each generator rather than evaluation noise, and constitutes the primary theoretical contribution of the work.

</details>


### [54] [Bounding the Causal Impact of ML-assisted Decision-Making via Counterfactual Correctness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21806)
*Jonathan Zhang,Erik Skalnes,Jacob Chen,Michael Oberst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种部分识别方法，利用先前随机对照试验（RCT）数据，通过两个单调性假设（反事实正确性和子组预测性能与结果的关系）来构建新机器学习模型因果效应的边界，避免重复进行昂贵的RCT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在高风险领域（如医疗、刑事司法）中频繁更新，但每次更新都进行新的随机对照试验（RCT）来评估其因果影响是不切实际的。因此需要一种方法，利用已有的RCT数据来评估新模型的因果效应。

Method: 采用部分识别（partial-identification）方法，利用两个单调性假设：(1) 个体层面的"反事实正确性"——正确预测导致不劣于错误预测的结果；(2) 子组预测性能与结果之间的关系——可解释为对模型输出的信任假设。

Result: 通过模拟研究证明，该方法相较于先前工作能够产生更具信息量的边界估计。

Conclusion: 利用细粒度预测准确性与下游结果之间的关联假设，可以在无需重复RCT的情况下，对新模型的因果效应进行有意义的推断，为模型更新后的评估提供可行方案。

Abstract: Predictive machine learning (ML) models are increasingly used to aid human decision-makers across various high-risk domains such as healthcare and criminal justice. There is a growing recognition of the need to evaluate the causal impact of deploying these systems on downstream outcomes, such as patient survival or crime recidivism. Randomized control trials (RCTs) can provide high-quality evidence on the impact of a deployed model, but they run into a challenge: it is often infeasible to run repeated trials when models are updated or retrained to improve predictive performance. In this work, we present a partial-identification approach to using prior RCT data to construct bounds on the causal effect of a new model. The core innovation in our approach is to leverage assumptions relating fine-grained predictive accuracy to downstream outcomes. We do so via two monotonicity assumptions: first, on individual-level `counterfactual correctness' (all else being equal, a correct prediction leads to non-inferior outcomes); and second, on the relation between subgroup predictive performance and outcomes, interpretable as an assumption regarding trust in model outputs. We demonstrate our method with a simulation study, illustrating how incorporating this information can lead to more informative bounds compared to prior work.

</details>


### [55] [Data eccentricity, asymptotics of Gaussian RBF reproducing kernel Hilbert space, and kernel PCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21823)
*Sergio A. Alvarez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，在高斯RBF核的大带宽极限下，其再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）在等向缩放意义下渐近等距于欧几里得空间，从而高斯CKA和核PCA的行为趋近于线性核与经典PCA，收敛速度由几何离心率参数ρ控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究高斯RBF核在大带宽极限下与线性核之间的渐近等价关系，为理解核方法的几何性质提供理论基础。

Method: 理论分析证明高斯RBF核的RKHS与欧氏空间在大带宽极限下的渐近等距性，并推导CKA和核PCA的收敛行为；通过ρ（最大与中位数成对距离之比）量化收敛速度，并用多数据集实验验证。

Result: 高斯RBF核的特征嵌入和PCA主方向渐近收敛到线性核/经典PCA的结果，残差为O((ρ/σ)²)；ρ是预测数据集特定收敛行为的可靠指标。

Conclusion: 大带宽下高斯RBF核与线性核渐近等价，ρ为收敛速度的简单有效预测因子。

Abstract: We show that, up to isotropic scaling, the Gaussian RBF reproducing kernel Hilbert space (RKHS) is asymptotically isometric to Euclidean space in the large bandwidth limit. This strongly suggests that kernel-based constructions reliant on metric properties of the RKHS will yield results for Gaussian RBF kernels that similarly approach those of linear kernels for large bandwidths. The asymptotic behavior of Gaussian CKA can be understood in this light. We further consider kernel PCA, showing that Gaussian RBF eigenvalues, eigenprojections, and principal components all converge to those of classical (linear) PCA as bandwidth $σ\rightarrow \infty$. For a given data representation, both the RKHS feature embeddings and the orthogonal PCA eigenframes of the two kernel types differ asymptotically by a geometric similarity transformation, up to a residual of size $O \left (\fracρσ \right )^2$, where $ρ$ is a measure of geometric eccentricity of the representation, equal to the ratio of maximum to median pairwise distance between data examples. Experiments over a diverse collection of data sets demonstrate that $ρ$ provides a simple and reliable predictor of dataset-specific convergence behavior in the top principal directions.

</details>


### [56] [A Graph-Based Control Interface for Traffic Signals on Heterogeneous Road Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21831)
*Bertil Braun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于共享图神经网络的交通信号控制接口，使用确定性关联矩阵将运动评分转换为信号相位，PPO实验验证了其在未见几何结构和异构城市图上的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通信号控制方法在图结构和动作空间上依赖固定参数形状，难以泛化到不同几何结构和规模的交通网络。

Method: 通过共享图神经网络为交通运动评分，结合确定性关联矩阵将评分转换为可变大小的合法信号相位；使用有向走廊节点提供上下文，运动节点表示受控路径；采用类型均值聚合生成标量，相位定义和信号时序保持在网络外部。

Result: 在未见合成网格几何结构上保持性能，但信号覆盖率变化暴露了分布偏移的敏感性；单个城市策略在五个异构城市图上产生异构结果。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了跨图结构迁移的可行性证据，但尚不能推广到任意道路网络。

Abstract: We present a traffic-signal control interface in which a shared graph neural network assigns scores to individual traffic movements. Each junction converts these scores into its own variable-sized set of legal signal phases using a deterministic incidence matrix. Directed corridor nodes provide traffic context, while movement nodes represent controlled input-to-output paths through junctions. Typed mean aggregation produces one scalar per movement; phase definitions and signal timing remain outside the learned network. This makes graph size and junction-specific action count independent of the learned parameter shapes. PPO experiments evaluate the interface on unseen synthetic grid geometries, altered signal coverage, and five heterogeneous city graphs. The policies retained performance across unseen geometries within the synthetic grid family, while changes in signal coverage exposed sensitivity to a signal-coverage distribution shift. A single trained city-policy instance executed across all five city graphs, with heterogeneous outcomes. These results provide feasibility evidence rather than a general estimate of transfer to arbitrary road networks.

</details>


### [57] [Searching the Space of Feed-Forward Neural-Network Weight-Update Rules with Fixed Depth Symbolic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21855)
*Charles Brum,Edward Finkelstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究探索符号回归能否自动发现比人工设计优化器更优的神经网络权重更新规则。在30个基准/网络组合中，25个案例发现了超越最优调参优化器的规则，MSE平均降低44.47%。发现规则形式多样，但多结合自适应归一化、动量、非线性变换和有理表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索符号回归能否自动发现比人工设计优化器（如Adam、SGD等）更优的神经网络权重更新规则，寻找一种轻量级的优化器自动发现机制。

Method: 将候选更新规则表示为固定深度的符号表达式，操作数来自常见优化器（梯度、动量、自适应梯度、矩估计等）。在小规模符号回归基准上，通过符号回归过程搜索权重更新规则，并与经过超参数调优的已有优化器进行比较。

Result: 在30个基准/网络组合中，符号回归发现的新规则在25个案例中优于最佳调参的已有优化器，改进案例的MSE平均降低44.47%。发现规则无统一符号形式，但常见特征包括自适应归一化、动量类量、非线性变换和有理表达式。

Conclusion: 符号回归可作为发现紧凑优化器变体的轻量级机制，但需更大规模验证。

Abstract: We investigate whether symbolic regression can discover explicit neural network weight-update rules that outperform standard hand-designed optimizers on small symbolic regression benchmarks. Candidate update rules are represented as fixed-depth symbolic expressions over operands derived from common optimizers, including gradient, momentum, adaptive-gradient, and moment-estimate quantities. Across 30 benchmark/neural network combinations, the symbolic regression procedure found an update rule outperforming the best hyperparameter-tuned established optimizer in 25 cases, with an aggregate MSE reduction of 44.47\% over the improved cases. The discovered rules do not all share a single common symbolic form, but many combine adaptive normalization, momentum-like quantities, nonlinear transformations, and rational expressions. These results suggest that symbolic regression can serve as a lightweight mechanism for discovering compact optimizer variants, while also highlighting the need for larger-scale validation.

</details>


### [58] [LeAct: Learning to Reason from Expert Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21856)
*Ziran Yang,Chengshuai Shi,Raj Ghugare,Benjamin Eysenbach,Karthik Narasimhan,Chi Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LeAct框架，通过将专家系统的行动作为潜在变量，反向恢复链式思维（CoT）推理过程，从而将专家知识蒸馏到学生模型中，在不完美信息博弈和机器人基准上显著优于直接模仿和专家迭代基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家系统（如游戏引擎、规划器、定理证明器）能产生接近最优的行动，但不会记录背后的推理过程（CoT）。现有推理数据依赖人工标注或从更强LLM蒸馏，而专家系统这一丰富监督源未被利用。

Method: 将CoT视为潜在变量，提出LeAct框架：学生模型为每个专家行动采样候选CoT，只保留那些能明显提升学生自身恢复该行动概率的CoT，从而优化潜在变量。

Result: 在小规模可枚举游戏中达到求解器数值下限；大规模场景下比最强专家迭代基线接近求解器5倍；在Flop Hold'em（约10^9信息集）中正面交锋胜出+60 mbb/g；在机器人探针任务中是唯一优于直接模仿的训练方法。

Conclusion: 专家系统可以成为基础模型全新的推理教师来源，LeAct提供了原则性框架来挖掘这一潜力。

Abstract: Modern reasoning models depend on reasoning data, today sourced from human annotations or distilled from stronger LLMs. However, a rich and largely untapped source of supervision lies in expert systems (e.g., game engines, classical planners, theorem provers), which routinely produce near-optimal actions across diverse domains. But these experts are silent: they commit to an action without writing down the chain of thought (CoT) behind it. Recovering that CoT as natural-language reasoning would distill expert knowledge into a student that generalizes beyond the demonstrated actions. We treat it as a latent variable and study how to recover it from the action alone. Our approach, LeAct (Learning to reason from Actions), optimizes this latent variable: the student samples candidate CoTs for each expert action, and we retain those that measurably improve its own probability of recovering the action. Across imperfect-information games at multiple scales and a simulated robotics benchmark, LeAct reaches the solver's numerical floor on small enumerable games. At larger scale, it is $5\times$ closer to the solver than the strongest expert-iteration baseline. At Flop Hold'em ($\sim 10^9$ infosets), LeAct wins head-to-head by $+60$ mbb/g, and on the robotics probe it is the only training recipe that improves on direct imitation. We present a principled framework and the result: expert systems become a categorically new source of reasoning teachers for foundation models.

</details>


### [59] [Scaling Laws for Classical Machine Learning on Tabular Data: A Benchmark Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21866)
*Kaihua Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 127名学生通过分布式课堂复现实验，在18个表格数据集和6种模型上进行了11,536次训练，拟合了1,648条幂律学习曲线。主要发现：(1)幂律拟合良好（77.7%的单元R²>0.8），树集成模型表现最佳；(2)同一模型族内存在近似共享指数，但并非完全普适；(3)即使固定随机种子，不同实现间指数估计仍有显著差异（CV(b)=0.144），源于协议中未约束的预处理步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往学习曲线研究通常规模小（单曲线、单团队、少量数据集），且多聚焦于树、线性、核模型在小规模表格数据上的幂律拟合。本文旨在通过大规模分布式课堂复现，检验这些结论在更大规模和更多样化设置下的稳健性，并量化实现差异带来的不确定性。

Method: 127名学生按固定协议在18个表格分类/回归数据集上运行6种模型（Boosting、随机森林、SVM、线性/逻辑回归、Ridge、Lasso），共11,536次训练，拟合幂律曲线error(N)=aN^(-b)+c。通过共享指数分析、AIC比较、留一数据集验证等方法评估幂律拟合质量及模型族内可压缩性。

Result: (1)77.7%的单元R²>0.8，树集成模型在完整数据上占优（Boosting在50%数据集上最佳，随机森林33%）；(2)5/6模型族支持近似共享指数（R²差距<0.011），但Lasso失败（阴性对照），Ridge在留一验证中不稳定；(3)不同实现间指数变异系数平均0.144，源于预处理等未约束步骤。

Conclusion: 幂律学习曲线在更大规模复现中仍具适用性，模型族内存在近似可压缩性但非完全普适；实现差异带来的方差不可忽视，建议发布聚合曲线和实用数据需求表以支持实际应用。

Abstract: Prior classical-ML learning-curve work fits power laws to tree, linear, and kernel models on tabular data, but at small scale: typically one curve, one team, a handful of cells. We present a distributed classroom-scale replication: 127 students each ran a fixed protocol on 3 assigned datasets, drawn from 18 tabular classification and regression datasets and 6 model families (Boosting, Random Forest, SVM, Linear/Logistic, Ridge, Lasso), yielding 11,536 training runs and 1,648 fitted power-law curves of the form error(N) = a N^(-b) + c. Three findings. (1) Power laws fit: R^2 > 0.8 on 77.7% of cells, with tree ensembles dominating at full data (Boosting 50% of datasets, RandomForest 33%; linear models underperform on classification). (2) Approximate shared exponents within a model family: for 5 of 6 families, a single family-level exponent predicts each family's cross-dataset curves nearly as well as per-dataset exponents (R^2 gap < 0.011), though AIC favors the unconstrained fit and curve collapse is partial (32-58% of points within +/-0.5 dex). We frame this as approximate predictive compressibility, not dataset-independent universality; Lasso fails outright (negative control) and Ridge is fragile under leave-one-dataset-out. (3) Replicator-implementation variance: with random_state=42 fixed, independent re-implementations of the same protocol still differ by mean CV(b) = 0.144 on the fitted exponent -- not seed variance, but the spread induced by unconstrained parts of the protocol (preprocessing, encoding, missing-value handling). We release the aggregated curves, per-cell fits, and a practical data-requirement table for N* to reach target error 0.15.

</details>


### [60] [Variance-Reduced Q-Learning over Static and Time-Varying Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21876)
*Sreejeet Maity,Feng Zhu,Aritra Mitra,Robert W. Heath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为VRDQ的分布式Q-learning算法，该算法通过epoch-based设计和共识协议实现多智能体协作学习最优值函数，在静态和时变网络上均取得了线性加速的收敛速率，且仅需$\tilde{O}(1)$的通信成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多个智能体在相同MDP上通过通信网络协作学习的分布式强化学习问题，旨在提升学习效率并降低通信开销。

Method: 提出VRDQ算法，采用epoch-based框架，在每个epoch内智能体本地估计Bellman最优性算子，并通过基于共识的协议进行信息扩散。

Result: 在静态和时变网络上均建立了高概率有限时间收敛速率，并证明了协作带来的线性加速效果；样本复杂度的加速仅需$\tilde{O}(1)$的通信量，显著优于先前工作。

Conclusion: VRDQ算法在分布式Q-learning中实现了高效的通信-样本权衡，证明了极低的通信成本即可获得线性加速。

Abstract: We investigate a decentralized reinforcement learning problem involving multiple agents that interact with the same Markov Decision Process (MDP). The agents can exchange information over a network to collectively learn the optimal state-action value function. For this setting, we introduce a novel epoch-based distributed $Q$-learning algorithm called VRDQ, where within each epoch, agents locally estimate the Bellman optimality operator and diffuse information using a consensus-based protocol. For both static and time-varying networks, we establish high-probability finite-time convergence rates for VRDQ that enjoy linear speedups from collaboration. Crucially, we prove that such speedups in sample-complexity require only $\tilde{O}(1)$ communication, substantially improving upon the communication costs in prior work.

</details>


### [61] [Remedying Coarsening-Based GNN Training under Heterophily via Adaptive Complementary Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21885)
*Guoming Li,Jian Yang,Xukun Wang,Zixiao Wang,Shangsong Liang,Yifan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ACE方法，通过自适应互补增强策略解决现有粗化训练方法在异配图上的性能退化问题，在保持同配图竞争力的同时显著提升异配图表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于粗化的图神经网络训练方法几乎仅在同配图上评估，在更具挑战性的异配图上性能严重退化，理论和实验均表明粗化过程中不可避免的信息丢失是主要原因。

Method: ACE是一种即插即用、模型无关的策略：(1) 学习投影仪重构原始节点特征；(2) 采用各向异性结构正则化嵌入局部异配性；(3) 利用同方差不确定性加权自适应平衡粗化图训练损失和全图辅助损失。

Result: 在异配基准上取得一致提升，同时在同配图上保持竞争性结果，计算开销极小。

Conclusion: ACE有效解决了粗化训练在异配图上的信息丢失问题，是一种通用且高效的增强策略。

Abstract: Coarsening-based training for graph neural networks (GNNs), i.e.\ training on coarsened graphs rather than the original large ones, has become a promising direction for scaling GNNs to massive graphs. However, prior work has been evaluated almost exclusively on \textit{homophilic} graphs, leaving the more challenging \textit{heterophilic} settings underexplored. We show, both empirically and theoretically, that existing coarsening-based training methods suffer significant performance degradation on heterophilic graphs due to inevitable loss of graph information during coarsening. To address this, we propose {\bf A}daptive {\bf C}omplementary {\bf E}nhancement, a plug-and-play, model-agnostic strategy that reintegrates the information discarded in coarsening: ACE learns a projector for re-constructing original node features and applies \textit{anisotropic structural regularization} to embed local heterophily. We further adopt \textit{homoscedastic uncertainty weighting} to adaptively balance the combined training objective of primary coarsened-graph training loss and full-graph auxiliary loss with augmented node features re-constructed by the heterophily-aware projector. Extensive experiments show that ACE drives consistent gains on heterophilic benchmarks while preserving competitive results on homophilic graphs with minimal computational overhead. Code is available at the GitHub repository: https://github.com/vasile-paskardlgm/ACE.

</details>


### [62] [MissHyper: Restoring Clinical Synchronicity in Missingness-Guided Hypergraph Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21922)
*Mingyi Ma,Qingxiong Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MissHyper模型，通过在消息传播前恢复共时间戳上下文，解决临床不规则多变量时间序列中事件初始化的表征瓶颈问题，在多个医疗数据集上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件中心模型过早扁平化共时间戳结构，导致同一时间戳的测量被孤立嵌入，局部患者状态上下文在后续消息传播层才能获得，形成了预传播表征瓶颈。

Method: 提出MissHyper模型：(1) 用局部支持密度线索增强每个事件；(2) 聚合共时间戳记录恢复患者状态上下文；(3) 用缺失引导门自适应融合节点证据与恢复的上下文信息。

Result: 在PhysioNet 2012、MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV数据集上，MissHyper在多步预测中均取得一致提升，优于强超图基线方法。

Conclusion: 改进事件初始化能够有效提升稀疏临床预测性能，无需重新设计下游传播架构；快照恢复、自适应融合和支持密度编码三个组件均发挥重要作用，事件初始化是稀疏临床预测的关键设计维度。

Abstract: Clinical irregular multivariate time series are shaped not only by physiological dynamics but also by the measurement process that determines when and what to observe. In event-centric models, however, co-timestamp structure can be flattened too early: measurements acquired at the same timestamp are embedded as isolated nodes, leaving local patient-state context unavailable until later message-passing layers. We study this pre-propagation representation bottleneck and address it by restoring co-timestamp context before message passing begins. We propose MissHyper, a missingness-guided hypergraph forecasting model with pre-propagation synchronicity restoration. MissHyper augments each event with a local support-density cue, aggregates co-timestamp records to recover patient-state context, and uses a missingness-guided gate to adaptively fuse node-specific evidence with the recovered context. Across PhysioNet 2012, MIMIC-III, and MIMIC-IV, MissHyper achieves consistent gains in multi-step forecasting and outperforms a strong hypergraph baseline. These results suggest that improving event initialization can benefit sparse clinical forecasting without requiring a redesigned downstream propagation architecture. Ablations indicate that snapshot restoration, adaptive fusion, and support-density encoding all contribute, pointing to event initialization as a critical design axis for sparse clinical forecasting.

</details>


### [63] [RIS-Kernel: A Model-Agnostic Architecture for Long-Context LLM Inference via Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21927)
*Anderson R. Santos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RIS推理引擎通过将自注意力复杂度从O(N²)降低到O(N log N)，使得大语言模型的长上下文文档分析（高达65,536 tokens）能在普通CPU服务器（16-128 GB RAM）上运行，无需GPU加速，且在部分设置下性能超越原生密集注意力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全自注意力机制的O(N²)复杂度限制了LLM处理长上下文（如65,536 tokens）的能力，且需要昂贵的GPU集群，亟需一种模型无关、资源友好的高效推理方案。

Method: 提出RIS推理引擎，一种模型无关的架构，无需修改权重即可通过稀疏随机几何将自注意力复杂度降至O(N log N)。包括两种变体：RIS-Stochastic（随机采样）和RIS-Structural（结构化采样）。

Result: 在32,768 tokens下，RIS-Stochastic在1%密度+70个种子时准确率75.00%（超过原生密集注意力的71.88%），5%密度+10个种子时持平（71.88%）。在65,536 tokens下，RIS相比零上下文基线（51.56%）提升高达14.06个百分点（p=0.078）。

Conclusion: 稀疏注意力可作为正则化器，低密度多种子能过滤噪声、提升性能；RIS使得长上下文LLM推理在标准学术硬件（无GPU）上可行。

Abstract: Full self-attention in large language models scales as O(N^2), which limits long-context document analysis to 65,536 tokens and requires costly GPU clusters. The Reduced Interaction Sampling (RIS) inference engine addresses this constraint as a model-agnostic architecture. Without modifying weights, RIS reduces self-attention complexity to O(N log N) using sparse stochastic geometry that fits within commodity memory limits. We validate RIS on Qwen2-1.5B-Instruct across two regimes. In controlled evaluations at 32,768 tokens (where native dense attention serves as the upper bound), RIS-Stochastic at 1% density and 70 ensemble seeds achieves 75.00% accuracy, outperforming the native dense baseline (71.88%), while RIS-Stochastic at 5% density and 10 seeds matches it (71.88%). This demonstrates that sparse attention acts as a regularizer: low density (1%) over multiple seeds filters out sequence-level noise, whereas higher density (5%) reintroduces distractor noise. Under the tightest budget, RIS-Structural reaches 68.75% accuracy at 1% density with just 10 seeds, recovering 75% of the contextual gap relative to the zero-context floor (59.38%). At 65,536 tokens, where dense attention triggers out-of-memory faults, RIS yields retrieval gains of up to 14.06 percentage points over the zero-context floor (51.56%), which is confirmed as marginally significant under McNemar's paired test (p = 0.078 < 0.10). All evaluations run on commodity, unaccelerated CPU servers (16-128 GB of RAM), demonstrating that long-context LLM inference is feasible on standard academic hardware without GPU acceleration.

</details>


### [64] [LatentFlow: Visual Analytics for Latent Space Analysis in Molecular Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21941)
*Shiyi Liu,Jiaqing Chen,Nicholas Hadler,Rostyslav Hnatyshyn,Michael W. Mahoney,Talita Perciano,John F. Hartwig,Gunther H. Weber,Ross Maciejewski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LatentFlow，一个用于分析分子图神经网络潜在空间的可视分析系统，通过改进的桑基图追踪聚类随层和模型状态的变化，帮助科学家理解潜在空间演化、识别有意义的分子模式并解释模型行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以跨层、跨不同模型状态（如训练轮次、模型配置和输入数据）分析潜在空间，导致科学家难以理解潜在空间如何演化以及它们与化学概念之间的关系。

Method: LatentFlow将嵌入向量聚类，利用改进的桑基图追踪聚类在层间和模型状态间的变化，并将聚类与代表性分子及其共享子结构关联，同时允许科学家引入领域知识与潜在空间模式进行比较。

Result: 通过两个案例研究，LatentFlow帮助科学家理解潜在空间演化、识别有意义的分子模式，并更好地解释模型行为。

Conclusion: LatentFlow有效支撑了化学和材料科学家对分子GNN潜在空间的分析需求，为理解模型内部化学信息组织方式提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Chemists and materials scientists increasingly use machine learning models, such as graph neural networks (GNNs), to predict properties of molecules and the outcomes of their reactions. Beyond predictive performance, understanding how these models organize chemical information internally in their latent spaces, i.e., the embeddings of the molecules, is critical. Analyzing latent spaces helps diagnose model behavior and assess whether the learned embeddings are organized in ways that reflect meaningful chemical relationships. Unfortunately, existing methods provide limited support for analyzing latent spaces across layers and across different model states (e.g., training epochs, model configurations, and input data), making it difficult to understand how these latent spaces evolve throughout a model or relate to chemical concepts. We present LatentFlow, a visual analytics system developed in collaboration with a domain expert for analyzing latent spaces in molecular GNNs. LatentFlow groups embeddings into clusters and supports exploration of latent spaces by tracking how these clusters change across layers and model states using a modified Sankey diagram. To support interpretation, LatentFlow links these clusters to representative molecules and their shared substructures, and it allows scientists to introduce their own domain knowledge and compare it with the patterns found in the latent spaces. We evaluate LatentFlow through two case studies. The results show that LatentFlow helps scientists understand how latent spaces evolve, identify meaningful molecular patterns, and better interpret model behavior.

</details>


### [65] [Multi-Agent Debate and Visual Information Extraction for SeePhys Pro: A 1st-Place Technical Report from ICML 2026 AI4Math Track 3 Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21946)
*Jiseok Kwak,Suhyeon Jo,Taewoo Kim,Yeongmin Kim,Byeonghu Na,Il-chul Moon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两阶段框架，先通过视觉信息提取将图像内容转化为文本，再通过多智能体辩论整合三个求解器，在SeePhys Pro物理问题回答任务中取得第一名（准确率0.802/0.743）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在处理视觉物理问题时存在显著的模态差距——当关键信息位于图中而非文本中时，模型性能大幅下降，且随着问题更多内容迁移到图像中，这种差距会进一步扩大。

Method: 两阶段框架：1) 视觉信息提取阶段，将图像内容重新表达为求解器可读的文本，以弥合模态差距；2) 推理阶段，通过多智能体辩论协调三个异构求解器进行答案选择。

Result: 在公开测试集上，整体准确率从单智能体基线的0.643提升至0.802；在私有测试集上整体准确率为0.743，获得公开榜和私有榜双料第一名。

Conclusion: 协调机制带来的提升主要来自可靠的答案选择而非额外的辩论过程；图像辅助的价值与问题中嵌入图像的信息量成正比。

Abstract: This technical report presents our approach to Challenge Track~3: SeePhys Pro at the 3rd AI for Math Workshop, where the task is to answer college-level physics questions whose statement and figure may be given partly or entirely as an image. Visual physics problems become substantially harder for large language models when the decisive information resides in a figure rather than in the text, and this modality gap widens as more of the problem migrates into the image. We address the task with a two-stage framework: a visual information extraction stage that re-expresses figure content as solver-readable text to close the modality gap, and a reasoning stage that orchestrates three heterogeneous solvers through multi-agent debate. Our analysis yields two findings: the gain from orchestration comes from reliable answer selection rather than from additional debate, and the value of a figure aid scales with how much of the problem is locked inside the image. The resulting pipeline improves overall accuracy over a single-agent baseline from 0.643 to 0.802 on the public split, and won 1st place on both the public and the private leaderboard (private overall 0.743).

</details>


### [66] [MA-DAR: Manifold-Aligned Dynamic Adaptive Routing for Continual Temporal Knowledge Graph Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21949)
*Xiangjun Shi,Chong Mu,Jinchuan Zhang,Lizong Zhang,Yuefeng He,Shang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MA-DAR框架，通过流形对齐和动态自适应路由解决持续时序知识图谱推理中的表示冲突问题（范数支配和语义模糊），有效提升重放式持续学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有重放式持续学习方法主要关注"重放什么"，而忽视了"如何融合重放表示与当前表示"，导致范数支配和语义模糊两种表示冲突，降低了持续推理性能。

Method: 提出MA-DAR框架：1）流形对齐：将重放表示和当前表示对齐到共享流形空间，缓解分布差异；2）动态门控机制：学习维度级融合权重，自适应确定表示贡献；3）极化正则化：鼓励更果断的路由决策，抑制模糊门控。

Result: 在四个公开持续TKG基准上，MA-DAR一致提升多种代表性TKG编码器的性能，在不同重放设置下均有效。消融和可视化分析验证了流形对齐和动态自适应路由缓解表示冲突的有效性。

Conclusion: MA-DAR是一种轻量级即插即用框架，通过解决表示冲突显著提升持续时序知识图谱推理性能，具有通用性和有效性。

Abstract: Continual temporal knowledge graph (TKG) reasoning aims to continuously incorporate newly emerging facts while preserving previously acquired knowledge. Replay-based continual learning has achieved promising performance by revisiting historical representations. However, existing methods primarily focus on what to replay, while largely overlooking how replayed representations should be integrated with current ones. Such direct integration often gives rise to two critical forms of representation conflict: \textit{norm domination} and \textit{semantic blurring}, ultimately degrading continual reasoning performance. To address these challenges, we propose MA-DAR (Manifold-Aligned Dynamic Adaptive Routing), a lightweight plug-and-play framework for replay representation fusion. MA-DAR first aligns replayed and current representations onto a shared manifold to alleviate distribution discrepancies. It then employs a dynamic gating mechanism to learn dimension-wise fusion weights, adaptively determining the contribution of replayed and current representations to the fused representation. Furthermore, a polarization regularizer encourages more decisive routing behaviors by discouraging ambiguous gating decisions, resulting in more stable and effective knowledge integration. Extensive experiments on four public continual TKG benchmarks demonstrate that MA-DAR consistently improves the performance of representative TKG encoders while remaining effective under different replay settings. Comprehensive ablation studies and visualization analyses further verify the effectiveness of manifold alignment and dynamic adaptive routing in mitigating representation conflicts and improving continual reasoning.

</details>


### [67] [Teaching LLMs to Self-Evolve: Cultivating Core Meta-Skills with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21971)
*Shujin Wu,Cheng Qian,Xiusi Chen,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MetaEvolve框架，通过数据合成、进化感知强化学习和推理时进化搜索，显式培养智能体在迭代自我进化中的元技能（如自我反思与环境反馈），在代码生成任务上显著超越基线模型，展示了培养元技能对增强AI自主进化能力的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我进化框架（如AlphaEvolve）的成功依赖于元技能（如带环境反馈的自我反思），但传统后训练方法忽略了这些元技能的培养。

Method: 提出MetaEvolve框架，包含三部分：1) 数据合成管道生成进化轨迹训练数据；2) 使用基于可验证奖励的强化学习训练模型；3) 推理时使用进化搜索进行多轮改进。

Result: 在7个编码基准上，分布内任务比最强基线提升10.01%，分布外任务提升24.12%；在开放算法优化问题上相对提升46.9%。

Conclusion: 显式培养自我进化元技能是实现更强大、更自主的AI进化能力的有效路径。

Abstract: Test-time scaling through iterative self-evolution with environment feedback, as demonstrated by AlphaEvolve, shows remarkable performance gains. We hypothesize that the success of such evolution frameworks hinges on meta-skills, such as self-reflection with environment feedback, that enable effective multi-round refinement, yet are largely neglected by traditional post-training. To bridge this gap, we present MetaEvolve, a framework designed to develop these meta-skills via a data synthesis pipeline, evolution-aware reinforcement learning (RL), and inference-time evolutionary search. Concretely, we ground MetaEvolve in coding, where program execution provides natural, continuous reward signals beyond binary correctness. Building on these signals, we synthesize evolution trajectories as training data, each containing a current program, its fitness score (combining correctness and efficiency), and a history of prior attempts, and train the model via RL with verifiable rewards derived from test case execution. By training on large-scale code data, we aim to inspire generalizable domain-agnostic meta-skills that can transfer broadly to open-ended problems where such rich training signals are scarce. Across seven coding benchmarks, MetaEvolve outperforms the strongest baseline by 10.01% absolute on in-distribution tasks and 24.12% on out-of-distribution tasks. On open-ended algorithm optimization problems entirely outside the training domain, it further achieves a 46.9% relative improvement. These results demonstrate that explicitly cultivating self-evolution meta-skills offers a principled path toward more capable and autonomously self-evolving AI.

</details>


### [68] [On the Convergence of Stochastic Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21975)
*Ru Wang,Chengchang Liu,John C. S. Lui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了低秩适配（LoRA）的优化理论，将确定性场景下的梯度复杂度从指数级改进为多项式级O(ε^{-4})，并提出两种随机优化方法LoRA-NSGDM（O(ε^{-8})复杂度）和LoRA-STORM（O(ε^{-6})复杂度）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LoRA分析仅给出指数级复杂度上界（exp{O(ε^{-2})}），过于宽松且不实用。本文旨在给出更紧的复杂度分析，并弥补随机场景下LoRA优化理论分析的空白。

Method: 通过更精细的收敛分析改进确定性LoRA-GD复杂度至O(ε^{-4})；提出LoRA-NSGDM（基于无偏随机梯度与有限方差假设），以及LoRA-STORM（结合方差缩减技术，利用均方光滑性条件）。

Result: 确定性场景：O(ε^{-4})次全梯度评估可达ε-驻点；随机场景：LoRA-NSGDM需O(ε^{-8})次随机梯度调用，LoRA-STORM进一步降至O(ε^{-6})。

Conclusion: 本文显著改进了LoRA优化理论的复杂度上界，为随机LoRA训练提供了理论保证，方差缩减策略能有效提升随机收敛效率。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) optimizes $J(B,A)=\mathcal L(W_\mathrm{base}+sBA)$ over two adapters $B \in \mathbb{R}^{m \times r}$ and $A \in \mathbb{R}^{r \times n}$ that form a low-rank update to a frozen pretrained weight matrix $W_\mathrm{base} \in \mathbb{R}^{m \times n}$. The prior analysis shows LoRA-GD takes $\exp\{\mathcal{O}(ε^{-2})\}$ oracle calls to find an $ε$-stationary point such that $\|\nabla J(B,A)\|\leq ε$ in the deterministic setting. We sharpen the analysis and show that $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-4})$ full-gradient evaluations suffice for the same first-order criterion. We further study stochastic LoRA under unbiased gradient estimates and finite variance. We propose LoRA-NSGDM, which finds an $ε$-stationary point with $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-8})$ stochastic oracle complexity. Under the additional mean-square smoothness condition, we use variance reduction strategy and propose LoRA-STORM, which improves the stochastic oracle complexity to $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-6})$.

</details>


### [69] [From Perturbation Correction to Geometry-Aware Sampling: Sharpness-Guided Equilibrium Sampling for Balanced Flat Minima in Long-Tailed Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21999)
*Jiaxin Deng,Junbiao Pang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Sharpness-Guided Equilibrium Sampling (SGS)，一种动态调整采样分布的方法，通过频率-锐度反馈机制平衡长尾学习中的类别暴露与优化几何，在不增加额外计算开销的情况下显著提升尾部类别的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长尾学习中，头部类别主导训练暴露，而尾部类别收敛到损失景观的尖锐区域，导致泛化能力差。现有重采样方法仅关注数据分布，忽略几何信息；而现有锐度感知最小化(SAM)方法只在有偏小批量采样后才修改损失或扰动。缺乏一种能够联合控制数据暴露与优化几何的主动采样机制。

Method: 将采样分布视为优化几何的主动控制变量，动态调整后续小批量：增加低频采样类的概率，同时抑制SAM诱导损失变化大的类别。仅使用累积类别计数和EMA锐度估计（来自标准SAM更新），无需类别级扰动或额外反向传播。通过连续时间随机微分方程和采样依赖的PAC-Bayes分析刻画该过程。

Result: 在CIFAR-100 LT（不平衡比100）上，SGS-SAM相比Focal-SAM尾部准确率提升10.85个百分点，总体提升3.56个百分点。在ImageNet-LT上，相比ImbSAM尾部提升6.59个百分点，总体提升1.20个百分点。训练时间仅为原始SAM的1.02倍。

Conclusion: SGS开辟了一条通过采样端控制损失景观的路径，表明未来长尾学习方法可以联合调节数据暴露与优化几何，而非将二者视为固定因素。

Abstract: Long-tailed learning couples two sources of poor generalization: head classes dominate training exposure, while under-represented classes often converge to sharper regions of the loss landscape. Conventional re-sampling addresses the former without considering geometry, whereas existing long-tailed sharpness-aware minimization (SAM) methods modify losses or perturbations only after biased mini-batches have been drawn. We introduce Sharpness-Guided Equilibrium Sampling (SGS), which treats the sampling distribution as an active control variable for optimization geometry. SGS dynamically adjusts subsequent mini-batches by increasing the sampling probability of less frequently sampled classes while suppressing classes with large SAM-induced loss changes, using only cumulative class counts and EMA sharpness estimates obtained from the standard SAM update, without class-wise perturbations or additional backward passes. We characterize this sampling process through a continuous-time stochastic differential equation and a sampling-dependent PAC-Bayes analysis, explaining how frequency-sharpness feedback can move training toward a more balanced flatness profile. On CIFAR-100 LT with an imbalance ratio of 100, SGS-SAM improves Focal-SAM by 10.85 points in tail accuracy and 3.56 points overall. On ImageNet-LT, it improves ImbSAM by 6.59 points on tail classes and 1.20 points overall. Its training time is only $1.02\times$ that of vanilla SAM. Beyond these gains, SGS establishes a sampling-side route to loss-landscape control, suggesting that future long-tailed methods can jointly regulate data exposure and optimization geometry rather than treating either as fixed.

</details>


### [70] [Energy Manifold Natural Gradient Descent: Riemannian Optimization for Neural PDE Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22004)
*Zhangyong Liang,Huanhuan Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EMNGD，一种用于参数位于黎曼流形上的物理信息和变分神经PDE求解器的流形优化框架，将能量自然梯度下降扩展到约束参数域，并证明了其收敛性和有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有能量自然梯度下降(ENGD)方法假设参数位于无约束的欧几里得域，无法处理参数受流形约束的情况，限制了其在物理信息和变分神经PDE求解器中的应用。

Method: 提出了EMNGD框架：1）将能量诱导的二次模型限制在可行切方向上；2）使用回缩操作保持优化过程中的参数约束；3）利用Woodbury恒等式将切向系统转换到样本空间；4）采用Nyström近似实现可扩展的样本空间求解。

Result: 在多个神经PDE基准测试上，EMNGD相比现有最优基线方法取得了更高的精度和更快的收敛速度。Woodbury保持EMNGD方向不变，可扩展求解器诊断量化了预条件和残差子采样的精度-成本权衡。

Conclusion: EMNGD在理论上具有坐标不变性、可退化为欧几里得空间的ENGD、具有Armijo回溯的全局一阶收敛性，并对不精确切向求解具有鲁棒性，为约束参数优化提供了有效的框架。

Abstract: Energy natural gradient descent (ENGD) aligns parameter updates with the curvature of an underlying function-space energy, but existing formulations assume an unconstrained Euclidean parameter domain. We introduce \EMNGDfull{}, a manifold optimization framework for physics-informed and variational neural PDE solvers whose parameters lie on a Riemannian manifold. EMNGD restricts the energy-induced quadratic model to feasible tangent directions and uses retractions to preserve parameter constraints throughout optimization. Under coercivity, we prove that the push-forward of the undamped EMNGD direction is the best feasible approximation to the function-space Newton vector in the energy metric. We establish coordinate invariance, exact reduction to ENGD in Euclidean space, global first-order convergence with Armijo backtracking, and robustness to inexact tangent solves. For quadratic residual energies and generalized Gauss--Newton pullbacks, the Woodbury identity transfers the tangent system to sample space without changing the direction. Nyström approximation provides scalable sample-space solves with controlled direction error and recovers the exact direction after iterative convergence. On the evaluated neural PDE benchmarks, EMNGD achieves higher accuracy and faster convergence than the compared state-of-the-art baselines. Woodbury preserves the EMNGD direction, while scalable-solver diagnostics quantify the accuracy--cost trade-off of preconditioning and residual subsampling.

</details>


### [71] [Cross-Domain Off-Policy Evaluation and Learning for Contextual Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22012)
*Yuta Natsubori,Masataka Ushiku,Yuta Saito*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出跨领域离策略评估与学习（Cross-Domain OPE/L）新框架，通过利用目标域和源域的日志数据，解决传统方法在少样本、确定性日志策略和新动作等场景下的方差大、探索不足问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OPE/L方法在少样本数据、确定性日志策略、新动作等实际常见场景中因方差问题或探索不足而失效，亟需一种能够利用多源历史数据的方法来应对这些挑战。

Method: 提出Cross-Domain OPE/L框架，同时利用目标域和来自其他领域（源域）的日志数据，并开发新型估计器和策略梯度方法，联合优化目标域的策略评估与学习。

Result: 在实证评估中，该方法在传统方法无法解决的场景下显著提升了OPE/L的性能。

Conclusion: 跨领域数据利用是突破当前OPE/L局限性、实现实际部署的有效途径。

Abstract: Off-Policy Evaluation and Learning (OPE/L) in contextual bandits is rapidly gaining popularity in real systems because new policies can be evaluated and learned securely using only historical logged data. However, existing methods in OPE/L cannot handle many challenging but prevalent scenarios such as few-shot data, deterministic logging policies, and new actions. In many applications, such as personalized medicine, content recommendations, education, and advertising, we need to evaluate and learn new policies in the presence of these challenges. Existing methods cannot evaluate and optimize effectively in these situations due to the notorious variance issue or limited exploration in the logged data. To enable OPE/L even under these unsolved challenges, we propose a new problem setup of Cross-Domain OPE/L, where we have access not only to the logged data from the target domain in which the new policy will be implemented but also to logged datasets collected from other domains. This novel formulation is widely applicable because we can often use historical data not only from the target hospital, country, device, or user segment but also from other hospitals, countries, devices, or segments. We develop a new estimator and policy gradient method to solve OPE/L by leveraging both target and source datasets, resulting in substantially enhanced OPE/L in the previously unsolved situations in our empirical evaluations.

</details>


### [72] [Cross-Tokenizer On-Policy Distillation via Byte-Prefix Marginalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22334)
*Hao Wang,Kun Yuan,Wenlin Zhong,Minglei Zhang,Han Xiao,Ming Sun,Honggang Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Byte-Prefix Marginalization（BPM）方法，通过共享字节空间将不同分词器的教师模型概率分布对齐到学生词汇表，实现跨分词器的全词汇蒸馏，在数学和编程任务上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同开源语言模型具有互补能力，希望将其知识蒸馏到紧凑的学生模型中。但现有跨分词器蒸馏方法会丢弃教师概率质量或将概率分配给语义无关的学生token。

Method: BPM在共享的字节空间中重新表达教师的下一个token分布：将每个教师token的概率分配给字节前缀匹配的最长学生token，聚合到相同学生token的质量，未匹配的质量放入显式的残差类别。

Result: 在Qwen3-32B、GLM-Z1-9B-0414、MiniMax-M2.7作为教师时，BPM在6个数学和编程基准上平均提升3.7-6.6个点，一致优于现有跨分词器方法。

Conclusion: BPM实现了词汇完整、字节对齐且质量保持的蒸馏目标，在超过99%的训练位置上精确恢复教师字节前缀边际分布，其余位置使用质量保持的下界近似，是一种有效的跨分词器蒸馏方案。

Abstract: Open-weight language models from different families exhibit complementary capabilities, motivating their consolidation into a compact student through on-policy distillation (OPD). However, full-vocabulary OPD typically assumes a shared tokenizer, while existing cross-tokenizer methods may discard teacher probability mass or assign it to student tokens with unrelated content. We introduce Byte-Prefix Marginalization (BPM), which re-expresses the teacher's next-token distribution over the student vocabulary in a shared byte space. Specifically, BPM assigns each teacher token's probability to the longest student token whose byte representation is a prefix of the teacher token's bytes, aggregates mass mapped to the same student token, and places otherwise unmatched mass in an explicit residual category. This produces a vocabulary-complete, byte-aligned, and mass-preserving target for dense OPD. The target exactly recovers the teacher-induced byte-prefix marginal when the relevant prefix does not span multiple teacher tokens (a condition satisfied at more than 99% of training positions) and uses a mass-preserving, chain-factorized lower bound otherwise. Across Qwen3-32B, GLM-Z1-9B-0414, and MiniMax-M2.7 as teachers, BPM consistently outperforms current cross-tokenizer methods on six mathematics and programming benchmarks, improving six-benchmark avg@8 by 3.7-6.6 points over the strongest baselines.

</details>


### [73] [DCS: A Unified Conditional Sensitivity Framework for Cross-Modal Copyright Infringement Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22035)
*Xiafeng Man*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于反事实条件分布偏移的版权侵权检测框架DCS，通过双分支条件敏感性统计量区分模型对特定训练数据的记忆与通用学习行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础模型常依赖或复制受版权保护的训练数据，但仅凭输出相似性不足以判定侵权（因为相似输出可能来自公共领域概念或通用统计泛化），亟需更精确的检测方法。

Method: 将版权侵权证据建模为反事实条件分布偏移，提出DCS框架：创建学习分支与遗忘分支，通过影响函数分析连接其位移与反事实重训练效果，并用校准检测统计量（减去正交条件下的敏感性）区分目标特异性记忆与通用微调不稳定性。

Result: DCS框架在岭回归线性模型、条件扩散模型、自回归语言模型和多模态模型上成功实例化，展示了通过预测差距、图像嵌入散度、token分布/熵变化及跨模态表示变化评估同一原理的效果。

Conclusion: 该框架为版权侵权检测提供了统一的理论基础和实践工具，能够有效区分模型对受保护内容的记忆与正常学习行为。

Abstract: Currently, most foundation models can reproduce or strongly depend on copyrighted training content, but output similarity alone is insufficient for infringement detection, because similar outputs may also arise from public-domain concepts, common stylistic conventions, or ordinary statistical generalization.
  In this paper, we develops a unified post-hoc detection framework that treats copyright infringement evidence as a counterfactual conditional distribution shift: a protected target is suspicious when the model's behavior under aligned conditions would change measurably if that target were included in, or removed from, the training process.
  We formalize this view through conditional differential privacy and introduce Dual-Branch Conditional Sensitivity (DCS), an operational statistic that measures the observable gap between two locally perturbed model states. Specifically, the proposed DCS framework creates a learning branch and an unlearning branch around the deployed model, connects their displacement to the unavailable counterfactual retraining effect through influence-function analysis, and bounds the observable sensitivity by the counterfactual privacy-budget surrogate, local curvature, training-set scale, and perturbation step size. To distinguish target-specific memorization from generic fine-tuning instability, we further define a calibrated detection statistic that subtracts the sensitivity measured under orthogonal conditions.
  The DCS framework is instantiated for ridge-regularized linear regression, conditional diffusion models, autoregressive language models, and multimodal models. These instantiations show how the same principle can be evaluated through prediction gaps, image-embedding divergence, token-distribution or entropy shifts, and cross-modal representation changes.

</details>


### [74] [CEL: Comprehensive Counterfactual Explanations Library and Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22045)
*Oleksii Furman,Łukasz Lenkiewicz,Marcel Musiałek,Maciej Zięba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CEL（反事实解释库），一个统一的反事实解释库和基准测试平台，包含18个数据集和14种主流方法，通过标准化协议实现公平、可重复的方法比较，填补了该领域缺乏系统性基准的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实解释研究在数据集划分、预测模型和评估指标上存在差异，导致方法间缺乏客观可比性，因此需要一个统一的标准化评估框架。

Method: 构建CEL库，整合18个多样化数据集，实现/重新实现14种反事实方法，并设计包含有效性、覆盖率、稀疏性、邻近性和分布合理性等多维度互补指标的统一评估协议。

Result: 首次在统一可重复框架下对多种反事实解释方法进行了全面定量比较，验证了标准化评估的有效性。

Conclusion: CEL基准测试旨在提升可重复性、实现公平比较，并为未来反事实解释方法的发展提供标准化工作台。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations are a prominent approach in explainable artificial intelligence (xAI), providing actionable guidance on what input changes would alter a model's prediction to a desired outcome. While early methods primarily focused on minimal feature changes, recent work incorporates additional properties such as sparsity, actionability and plausibility. Despite this progress, fair and systematic evaluation remains challenging. Existing studies often rely on different data splits, predictive models, and evaluation metrics, which limits objective comparison across methods.
